Pelican-Unified 1.0: A Unified Embodied Intelligence Model for Understanding, Reasoning, Imagination and Action
Authors: Yi Zhang, Yinda Chen, Che Liu, Zeyuan Ding, Jin Xu, Shilong Zou, Junwei Liao, Jiayu Hu, Xiancong Ren, Xiaopeng Zhang, Yechi Liu, Haoyuan Shi, Zecong Tang, Haosong Sun, Renwen Cui, Kuishu Wu, Wenhai Liu, Yang Xu, Yingji Zhang, Yidong Wang, Senkang Hu, Jinpeng Lu, Nga Teng Chan, Yechen Wu, Yong Dai, Jian Tang, Xiaozhu Ju
First: 2026-05-14T17:50:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:50:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present Pelican-Unified 1.0, the first embodied foundation model trained according to the principle of unification. Pelican-Unified 1.0 uses a single VLM as a unified understanding module, mapping scenes, instructions, visual contexts, and action histories into a shared semantic space. The same VLM also serves as a unified reasoning module, autoregressively producing task-, action-, and future-oriented chains of thought in a single forward pass and projecting the final hidden state into a dense latent variable. A Unified Future Generator (UFG) then conditions on this latent variable and jointly generates future videos and future actions through two modality-specific output heads within the same denoising process. The language, video, and action losses are all backpropagated into the shared representation, enabling the model to jointly optimize understanding, reasoning, imagination, and action during training, rather than training three isolated expert systems. Experiments demonstrate that unification does not imply compromise. With a single checkpoint, Pelican-Unified 1.0 achieves strong performance across all three capabilities: 64.7 on eight VLM benchmarks, the best among comparable-scale models; 66.03 on WorldArena, ranking first; and 93.5 on RoboTwin, the second-best average among compared action methods. These results show that the unified paradigm succeeds in preserving specialist strength while bringing understanding, reasoning, imagination, and action into one model.
中文标题/摘要
标题：鹈鹕统一1.0：面向理解、推理、想象与行动的统一具身智能模型
我们提出了鹈鹕统一1.0，这是首个依据统一原则训练的具身基础模型。鹈鹕统一1.0使用单一视觉语言模型（VLM）作为统一理解模块，将场景、指令、视觉上下文和行动历史映射到共享语义空间。同一VLM同时作为统一推理模块，在单次前向传播中自回归生成任务导向、行动导向和未来导向的思维链，并将最终隐藏状态投影为稠密潜在变量。随后，统一未来生成器（UFG）基于该潜在变量，在同一去噪过程中通过两个模态特定输出头联合生成未来视频与未来行动。语言、视频与行动损失均反向传播至共享表示，使模型在训练中联合优化理解、推理、想象与行动，而非训练三个独立专家系统。 实验表明，统一并不意味着妥协。单个检查点下，鹈鹕统一1.0在三项能力上均取得优异表现：在八个VLM基准测试中得分64.7，优于同类规模模型；在WorldArena上得分66.03，排名第一；在RoboTwin上得分93.5，为对比行动方法中的第二佳平均分。这些结果表明，统一范式成功保留了专家能力，同时将理解、推理、想象与行动整合于单一模型中。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of training separate models for understanding, reasoning, and action in embodied AI, Pelican-Unified 1.0 introduces a unified framework where a single vision-language model serves as both understanding and reasoning module, mapping multimodal inputs into a shared semantic space and generating future-oriented thought chains. A Unified Future Generator then jointly produces video and action outputs conditioned on the latent representation, with all modalities optimized through shared backpropagation. Experiments show that this unified approach achieves top-tier performance: 64.7 on VLM benchmarks, 66.03 on WorldArena, and 93.5 on RoboTwin, demonstrating that unification can preserve or even enhance specialist capabilities without sacrificing performance.
为解决传统方法中理解、推理、想象与行动需分别训练多个独立模型的低效问题，作者提出Pelican-Unified 1.0，该模型以单一视觉语言模型（VLM）同时承担理解与推理功能，将场景、指令、视觉上下文及动作历史映射至共享语义空间，并自回归生成任务、动作与未来导向的思维链。随后，统一未来生成器（UFG）基于该模型隐藏状态的稠密变量，通过两个模态专属输出头在单次去噪过程中联合生成未来视频与动作。语言、视频与动作损失均反向传播至共享表示中，实现四类能力的联合优化。实验表明，该统一架构未牺牲专业能力，单个模型在八项VLM基准上得分64.7（优于同类模型）、WorldArena上达66.03（排名第一）、RoboTwin上为93.5（排名第二），证明统一范式可同时保持专家级表现并整合多模态能力。
Widening the Gap: Exploiting LLM Quantization via Outlier Injection
Authors: Xiaohua Zhan, Kazuki Egashira, Robin Staab, Mark Vero, Martin Vechev
First: 2026-05-14T17:50:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:50:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM quantization has become essential for memory-efficient deployment. Recent work has shown that quantization schemes can pose critical security risks: an adversary may release a model that appears benign in full precision but exhibits malicious behavior once quantized by users. However, existing quantization-conditioned attacks have been limited to relatively simple quantization methods, where the attacker can estimate weight regions that remain invariant under the target quantization. Notably, prior attacks have consistently failed to compromise more popular and sophisticated schemes, limiting their practical impact. In this work, we introduce the first quantization-conditioned attack that consistently induces malicious behavior that can be triggered by a broad range of advanced quantization techniques, including AWQ, GPTQ, and GGUF I-quants. Our attack exploits a simple property shared by many modern quantization methods: large outliers can cause other weights to be rounded to zero. Consequently, by injecting outliers into specific weight blocks, an adversary can therefore induce a targeted, predictable weight collapse in the model. This effect can be used to craft seemingly benign full-precision models that exhibit a wide range of malicious behaviors after quantization. Through extensive evaluation across three attack scenarios and LLMs, we show that our attack achieves high success rates against a broad range of quantization methods on which prior attacks fail. Our results demonstrate, for the first time, that the security risks of quantization are not restricted to simpler schemes but are broadly relevant across complex, widely-used quantization methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：扩大差距：通过异常值注入利用大语言模型量化漏洞
大语言模型量化已成为内存高效部署的必要手段。近期研究表明，量化方案可能带来严重安全风险：攻击者可发布一个在全精度下看似无害的模型，但在用户量化后表现出恶意行为。然而，现有量化条件攻击仅限于相对简单的量化方法，攻击者可预估在目标量化下保持不变的权重区域。值得注意的是，此前攻击始终无法攻破更流行且复杂的量化方案，限制了其实际影响。本文首次提出一种可一致诱导恶意行为的量化条件攻击，适用于多种先进量化技术，包括AWQ、GPTQ和GGUF I-quants。该攻击利用现代量化方法的共同特性：大异常值可导致其他权重被舍入为零。因此，通过向特定权重块注入异常值，攻击者可诱导模型发生可预测的定向权重坍塌。该效应可用于构建看似无害的全精度模型，量化后表现出多种恶意行为。通过在三种攻击场景和多个大语言模型上的广泛评估，我们证明该攻击对先前攻击失效的多种量化方法均具有高成功率。我们的结果首次表明，量化安全风险不仅限于简单方案，而是广泛存在于复杂且广泛使用的量化方法中。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the security risks inherent in LLM quantization, this work introduces a novel attack that exploits a shared property among modern quantization methods: large outliers can force other weights to round to zero. The method injects carefully crafted outliers into specific weight blocks to induce predictable weight collapse after quantization, enabling the creation of seemingly benign full-precision models that exhibit malicious behavior when deployed with common quantization schemes like AWQ, GPTQ, and GGUF I-quants. Experimental results across three attack scenarios and multiple LLMs demonstrate high success rates against quantization methods that prior attacks could not compromise, proving that quantization-induced vulnerabilities are not limited to simple schemes but extend broadly to widely adopted, advanced quantization techniques.
鉴于大语言模型量化部署中的安全风险，本研究提出一种新攻击方法，利用现代量化方法共有的特性：大异常值可迫使其他权重归零。该方法通过在特定权重块中注入精心设计的异常值，诱导量化后权重坍塌，从而制造出看似无害的全精度模型，但在实际量化部署中表现出恶意行为。实验在三种攻击场景和多个大模型上验证，结果显示该攻击对AWQ、GPTQ、GGUF I-quants等主流量化方案均具有高成功率，证明量化引发的安全漏洞广泛存在于复杂且常用的量化方法中，而非仅限于简单方案。
Learning, Fast and Slow: Towards LLMs That Adapt Continually
Authors: Rishabh Tiwari, Kusha Sareen, Lakshya A Agrawal, Joseph E. Gonzalez, Matei Zaharia, Kurt Keutzer, Inderjit S Dhillon, Rishabh Agarwal, Devvrit Khatri
First: 2026-05-12T17:58:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:49:32+00:00
Comments: 29 pages, 14 figures, including appendix; Blog post: https://gepa-ai.github.io/gepa/blog/2026/05/11/learning-fast-and-slow/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are trained for downstream tasks by updating their parameters (e.g., via RL). However, updating parameters forces them to absorb task-specific information, which can result in catastrophic forgetting and loss of plasticity. In contrast, in-context learning with fixed LLM parameters can cheaply and rapidly adapt to task-specific requirements (e.g., prompt optimization), but cannot by itself typically match the performance gains available through updating LLM parameters. There is no good reason for restricting learning to being in-context or in-weights. Moreover, humans also likely learn at different time scales (e.g., System 1 vs 2). To this end, we introduce a fast-slow learning framework for LLMs, with model parameters as "slow" weights and optimized context as "fast" weights. These fast "weights" can learn from textual feedback to absorb the task-specific information, while allowing slow weights to stay closer to the base model and persist general reasoning behaviors. Fast-Slow Training (FST) is up to 3x more sample-efficient than only slow learning (RL) across reasoning tasks, while consistently reaching a higher performance asymptote. Moreover, FST-trained models remain closer to the base LLM (up to 70% less KL divergence), resulting in less catastrophic forgetting than RL-training. This reduced drift also preserves plasticity: after training on one task, FST trained models adapt more effectively to a subsequent task than parameter-only trained models. In continual learning scenarios, where task domains change on the fly, FST continues to acquire each new task while parameter-only RL stalls.
中文标题/摘要
标题：快速与慢速学习：迈向持续适应的大型语言模型
大型语言模型（LLMs）通常通过更新参数（如强化学习）来完成下游任务，但参数更新会迫使模型吸收特定任务信息，导致灾难性遗忘和可塑性丧失。相比之下，固定参数的上下文学习可廉价快速适应任务需求（如提示优化），但通常无法达到参数更新所能实现的性能增益。没有理由将学习限制在上下文或权重更新之间。此外，人类也可能在不同时间尺度上学习（如系统1与系统2）。为此，我们提出一种快慢学习框架，将模型参数视为“慢权重”，优化的上下文视为“快权重”。这些“快权重”可通过文本反馈学习吸收任务特定信息，同时使“慢权重”更贴近基础模型并保持通用推理能力。快慢训练（FST）在推理任务中比仅慢学习（RL）样本效率高3倍，且始终达到更高性能极限。此外，FST训练的模型更接近基础LLM（KL散度低至70%），灾难性遗忘更少。这种较小的漂移还保留了可塑性：在完成一个任务后，FST训练模型比仅参数训练模型更能有效适应后续任务。在持续学习场景中，当任务领域动态变化时，FST能持续学习新任务，而仅参数RL则停滞不前。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the trade-off between catastrophic forgetting in parameter updates and the limited adaptability of in-context learning, this work proposes a fast-slow learning framework for LLMs that separates task-specific adaptation (fast weights via context optimization) from general reasoning (slow weights preserved near base model). Experiments show that Fast-Slow Training (FST) achieves up to 3x sample efficiency over slow-only RL on reasoning tasks, reaches higher performance asymptotes, and maintains closer proximity to the base model (up to 70% less KL divergence), reducing forgetting and enhancing plasticity. In continual learning settings, FST models adapt more effectively to new tasks than parameter-only RL models, which often stall.
为解决参数更新导致的灾难性遗忘与上下文学习适应性不足的矛盾，本文提出Fast-Slow Training（FST）框架，将模型参数视为“慢权重”，优化的上下文视为“快权重”。该方法使快权重通过文本反馈学习任务特定信息，同时保持慢权重贴近基础模型以保留通用推理能力。实验表明，FST在推理任务中比仅参数更新的强化学习方法效率高3倍，收敛性能更高，且遗忘更少（KL散度降低70%）。更重要的是，FST模型保持更强的可塑性，在连续学习场景中能持续适应新任务，而仅参数更新的模型则趋于停滞。
Dynamic Mixed-Precision Routing for Efficient Multi-step LLM Interaction
Authors: Yuanzhe Li, Jianing Deng, Jingtong Hu, Tianlong Chen, Song Wang, Huanrui Yang
First: 2026-02-02T19:24:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:47:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance in long-horizon decision-making tasks through multi-step interaction and reasoning at test time. While practitioners commonly believe a higher task success rate necessitates the use of a larger and stronger LLM model, multi-step interaction with a large LLM incurs prohibitive inference cost. To address this problem, we explore the use of low-precision quantized LLMs in the long-horizon decision-making process. Based on the observation of diverse sensitivities among interaction steps, we propose Dynamic Mixed-Precision Routing (DMR), a framework that adaptively selects between high-precision and low-precision LLMs at each decision step. The router is trained via a two-stage pipeline, consisting of KL-divergence-based supervised learning that identifies precision-sensitive steps, followed by Group-Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to further improve task success rates. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop demonstrate that our approach achieves a strong accuracy-cost trade-off over single-precision baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：动态混合精度路由以实现高效多步大语言模型交互
大语言模型（LLMs）通过在测试时进行多步交互和推理，在长时程决策任务中表现出色。尽管业界普遍认为更高的任务成功率需要使用更大更强的LLM模型，但与大型LLM进行多步交互会产生高昂的推理成本。 为解决此问题，我们探索在长时程决策过程中使用低精度量化LLM。基于不同交互步骤对精度敏感性的观察，我们提出动态混合精度路由（DMR）框架，该框架在每个决策步骤中自适应地选择高精度或低精度LLM。路由器通过两阶段管道训练：第一阶段基于KL散度的监督学习识别精度敏感步骤；第二阶段采用组相对策略优化（GRPO）进一步提升任务成功率。在ALFWorld和WebShop上的实验表明，我们的方法相比单精度基线实现了更强的准确率-成本权衡。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high inference cost of using large LLMs in multi-step long-horizon tasks despite their strong performance, the paper proposes Dynamic Mixed-Precision Routing (DMR) to adaptively switch between high- and low-precision LLMs per decision step. The method trains a router via a two-stage pipeline: first using KL-divergence-based supervised learning to identify precision-sensitive steps, then refining it with Group-Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to maximize task success. Experimental results on ALFWorld and WebShop show that DMR achieves significantly better accuracy-cost trade-offs compared to single-precision baselines.
为在多步LLM交互中降低推理成本同时保持性能，本文提出动态混合精度路由（DMR）框架，根据各步骤对精度的敏感性自适应选择高或低精度模型。该方法分两阶段训练路由器：首先用KL散度识别敏感步骤，再用组相对策略优化提升任务成功率。在ALFWorld和WebShop上的实验表明，DMR相比单精度基线在准确率与成本间取得更优平衡。
Causal Forcing++: Scalable Few-Step Autoregressive Diffusion Distillation for Real-Time Interactive Video Generation
Authors: Min Zhao, Hongzhou Zhu, Kaiwen Zheng, Zihan Zhou, Bokai Yan, Xinyuan Li, Xiao Yang, Chongxuan Li, Jun Zhu
First: 2026-05-14T17:46:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:46:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Real-time interactive video generation requires low-latency, streaming, and controllable rollout. Existing autoregressive (AR) diffusion distillation methods have achieved strong results in the chunk-wise 4-step regime by distilling bidirectional base models into few-step AR students, but they remain limited by coarse response granularity and non-negligible sampling latency. In this paper, we study a more aggressive setting: frame-wise autoregression with only 1--2 sampling steps. In this regime, we identify the initialization of a few-step AR student as the key bottleneck: existing strategies are either target-misaligned, incapable of few-step generation, or too costly to scale. We propose \textbf{Causal Forcing++}, a principled and scalable pipeline that uses \emph{causal consistency distillation} (causal CD) for few-step AR initialization. The core idea is that causal CD learns the same AR-conditional flow map as causal ODE distillation, but obtains supervision from a single online teacher ODE step between adjacent timesteps, avoiding the need to precompute and store full PF-ODE trajectories. This makes the initialization both more efficient and easier to optimize. The resulting pipeline, \ours, surpasses the SOTA 4-step chunk-wise Causal Forcing under the \textit{\textbf{frame-wise 2-step setting}} by 0.1 in VBench Total, 0.3 in VBench Quality, and 0.335 in VisionReward, while reducing first-frame latency by 50\% and Stage 2 training cost by $\sim$$4\times$. We further extend the pipeline to action-conditioned world model generation in the spirit of Genie3. Project Page: https://github.com/thu-ml/Causal-Forcing and https://github.com/shengshu-ai/minWM .
中文标题/摘要
标题：因果强制++：面向实时交互式视频生成的可扩展少步自回归扩散蒸馏
实时交互式视频生成需要低延迟、流式和可控的生成过程。现有自回归（AR）扩散蒸馏方法在块级4步设置中通过将双向基础模型蒸馏为少步AR学生模型取得了优异效果，但受限于粗粒度响应和不可忽略的采样延迟。本文研究更激进的设置：帧级自回归仅需1-2步采样。在此设置中，我们发现少步AR学生模型的初始化是关键瓶颈：现有策略要么目标错位、无法支持少步生成，要么成本过高难以扩展。我们提出\textbf{Causal Forcing++}，一种原理清晰且可扩展的流程，利用\emph{因果一致性蒸馏}（causal CD）进行少步AR初始化。核心思想是：因果CD学习与因果ODE蒸馏相同的AR条件流映射，但仅从相邻时间步间的单个在线教师ODE步获取监督，无需预计算和存储完整PF-ODE轨迹。这使得初始化更高效且更易优化。所提出的流程\ours在\textit{\textbf{帧级2步设置}}下，相较SOTA块级4步Causal Forcing，在VBench Total上提升0.1、VBench Quality上提升0.3、VisionReward上提升0.335，同时首帧延迟降低50%，Stage 2训练成本降低约4倍。我们进一步将该流程扩展至Genie3精神下的动作条件世界模型生成。项目页面：https://github.com/thu-ml/Causal-Forcing 和 https://github.com/shengshu-ai/minWM。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for real-time, frame-level interactive video generation with minimal latency, this work addresses the limitations of existing few-step autoregressive diffusion distillation methods, which suffer from coarse granularity and high sampling cost. The authors propose Causal Forcing++, a scalable initialization technique using causal consistency distillation that leverages a single online teacher step between adjacent timesteps, avoiding expensive precomputed trajectories. Evaluated under a frame-wise 2-step setting, the method outperforms the state-of-the-art 4-step chunk-wise approach by 0.1 in VBench Total, 0.3 in VBench Quality, and 0.335 in VisionReward, while cutting first-frame latency by 50% and reducing Stage 2 training cost by approximately fourfold.
为实现低延迟、帧级交互式视频生成，本文针对现有少步自回归扩散蒸馏方法在细粒度响应和高采样成本方面的局限性，提出Causal Forcing++方法，通过因果一致性蒸馏初始化少步自回归学生模型，仅依赖相邻时间步间的单步在线ODE监督，避免预计算完整轨迹。实验表明，该方法在帧级2步生成设置下超越当前最优的4步块级方法，VBench Total提升0.1，Quality提升0.3，VisionReward提升0.335，同时首帧延迟降低50%，Stage 2训练成本降低约四倍。
Leveraging Speech to Identify Signatures of Insight and Transfer in Problem Solving
Authors: Linas Nasvytis, Judith E. Fan
First: 2026-05-13T04:04:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:46:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many problems seem to require a flash of insight to solve. What form do these sudden insights take, and what impact do they have on how people approach similar problems in the future? In this work, we prompted participants (N = 189) to think aloud as they attempted to solve a sequence of five "matchstick-arithmetic" problems. These problems either all relied on the same kind of non-obvious solution (Same group) or a different kind each time (Different group). We found that Same participants improved more rapidly than Different participants, and as they improved, they talked more and talked about different things when solving later problems. Specifically, they were more likely to spontaneously categorize the problem they were working on. Taken together, these findings suggest that a hallmark of transferable insights is their accessibility for verbal report, even if the underlying precursors of insight remain difficult to articulate.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用言语识别问题解决中顿悟与迁移的特征
许多问题似乎需要顿悟才能解决。这些突然的顿悟呈现什么形式，又如何影响人们未来解决类似问题的方式？本研究要求189名参与者在解决五个“火柴棒算术”问题时进行口头思维。这些问题要么每次都依赖同一种非显而易见的解法（相同组），要么每次解法不同（不同组）。我们发现，相同组参与者进步更快，且随着进步，他们在解决后续问题时言语更多、谈论的内容也更丰富，尤其更倾向于自发对当前问题进行分类。这些发现表明，可迁移的顿悟的一个显著特征是其可被口头报告，即使其背后的顿悟前兆仍难以言明。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how verbal speech during problem-solving can reveal signs of insight and transfer. Participants (N = 189) solved five matchstick-arithmetic problems while thinking aloud, with problems either consistently requiring the same non-obvious solution (Same group) or varying in solution type (Different group). Results showed that Same group participants improved faster and produced more diverse, self-categorizing verbalizations over time, suggesting that transferable insights are more readily verbalizable, even if their underlying precursors remain hard to articulate.
本研究旨在探究问题解决中突然的顿悟如何表现及其对未来表现的影响。189名参与者在解决五道火柴棒算术题时进行口头报告，题目要么全部依赖同一种非显而易见的解法（相同组），要么每次解法不同（不同组）。结果发现，相同组参与者进步更快，且随着能力提升，他们说话更多、更自发地对题目进行归类。这些口头报告行为与可迁移的顿悟相关，表明即使其内在前因难以言表，能被口头描述的顿悟更可能带来持久提升。
Forgetting That Sticks: Quantization-Permanent Unlearning via Circuit Attribution
Authors: Saisab Sadhu, Pratinav Seth, Vinay Kumar Sankarapu
First: 2026-05-14T17:44:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:44:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Standard unlearning evaluations measure behavioral suppression in full precision, immediately after training, despite every deployed language model being quantized first. Recent work has shown that 4-bit post-training quantization can reverse machine unlearning; we show this is not a tuning artefact but a systematic dual failure: gradient-based methods that achieve meaningful forgetting lose it under compression, while methods that survive quantization barely change the model. Both failures trace to the same root cause: across all baselines, per-parameter updates lie 47-828x below the NF4 quantization bin width; updates diffused across billions of parameters cannot clear quantization bin boundaries, a consequence we formalize as a sparsity-permanence tradeoff. We present MANSU (Mechanistic-Aligned Null-Space Unlearning), which resolves both modes by combining causal circuit attribution to isolate the minimal forget-set subgraph, circuit-restricted null-space projection with a diagonal-Fisher retain bound, and a per-parameter magnitude floor guaranteeing quantization survival by construction. We additionally introduce Circuit Attribution Divergence (CAD), a mechanistic verification metric distinguishing structural erasure from behavioral suppression, a distinction existing metrics cannot make. Across multiple model families and hazard benchmarks, MANSU is the first method to jointly satisfy all four properties with margin on each (meaningful forgetting, retain preservation, non-positive PTQ gap, and structural erasure), while gradient-based baselines recover up to +0.05 accuracy under compression.
中文标题/摘要
标题：遗忘之固：通过电路归因实现量化永久遗忘
标准遗忘评估在训练后立即以全精度测量行为抑制，而实际部署的语言模型均先进行量化。近期研究表明，4位后训练量化可逆转机器遗忘；我们证明这不是调参伪影，而是系统性双重失败：基于梯度的方法在压缩下会丧失有意义的遗忘能力，而能承受量化的算法模型几乎无变化。两种失败均源于同一根本原因：所有基线方法中，每个参数的更新量均低于NF4量化区间宽度47至828倍；在数十亿参数上扩散的更新无法跨越量化区间边界，我们将其形式化为稀疏性与永久性的权衡。我们提出MANSU（机制对齐零空间遗忘），通过结合因果电路归因以隔离最小遗忘子图、电路受限零空间投影与对角Fisher保留界，以及参数幅度下限保证量化生存，同时解决两种失败模式。此外，我们引入电路归因差异（CAD），一种机制验证指标，可区分结构性擦除与行为抑制，而现有指标无法做到这一点。在多个模型家族和风险基准测试中，MANSU是首个同时满足四项属性（有意义遗忘、保留保护、非正PTQ差距、结构性擦除）且每项均有余量的方法，而基于梯度的基线方法在压缩下最多恢复+0.05准确率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the failure of standard unlearning methods under post-training quantization, this work identifies a systematic dual failure: gradient-based methods lose forgetting after compression, while quantization-resilient methods fail to suppress behavior. The root cause is per-parameter updates being too small relative to quantization bin widths, preventing effective structural erasure. The authors introduce MANSU, a method combining causal circuit attribution, null-space projection with Fisher-bound constraints, and per-parameter magnitude flooring to ensure quantization compatibility and meaningful forgetting. Experimental results show MANSU is the first to simultaneously satisfy all four unlearning criteria—meaningful forgetting, retain preservation, non-positive PTQ gap, and structural erasure—with consistent margins across multiple models and benchmarks, while gradient-based baselines degrade accuracy under compression.
针对标准遗忘方法在量化后失效的问题，本文发现梯度方法在压缩下遗忘消失，而耐量方法则无法有效抑制行为。其根本原因是参数更新幅度远小于量化区间宽度，导致无法清除量化边界。作者提出MANSU方法，结合因果电路归因、电路受限零空间投影和参数幅度下限，确保遗忘与量化兼容。MANSU在多个模型上首次同时满足四项指标（有效遗忘、保留保护、非正PTQ差距、结构擦除），而梯度基方法在压缩下准确率反而下降达+0.05。
Training ML Models with Predictable Failures
Authors: Will Schwarzer, Scott Niekum
First: 2026-05-14T17:41:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:41:52+00:00
Comments: 32 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Estimating how often an ML model will fail at deployment scale is central to pre-deployment safety assessment, but a feasible evaluation set is rarely large enough to observe the failures that matter. Jones et al. (2025) address this by extrapolating from the largest k failure scores in an evaluation set to predict deployment-scale failure rates. We give a finite-k decomposition of this estimator's forecast error and show that it has a built-in bias toward over-prediction in the typical case, which is the safety-favorable direction. This bias is offset when the evaluation set misses a rare high-failure mode that the deployment set contains, leaving the forecast to under-predict at deployment scale. We propose a fine-tuning objective, the forecastability loss, that addresses this failure mode. In two proof-of-concept experiments, a language-model password game and an RL gridworld, fine-tuning substantially reduces held-out forecast error while preserving primary-task capability and achieving safety similar to that of supervised baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用可预测的失败训练机器学习模型
评估机器学习模型在部署规模下的失败频率是部署前安全评估的核心，但可行的评估集通常不足以观察到重要的失败情况。Jones 等人（2025）通过从评估集中最大的 k 个失败分数进行外推，预测部署规模的失败率。我们对该估计量的预测误差进行了有限 k 分解，并表明在典型情况下，该估计量存在固有的偏向于高估的偏差，这符合安全导向。当评估集遗漏了部署集中存在的罕见高失败模式时，该偏差会被抵消，导致预测在部署规模下低估。我们提出了一种微调目标——可预测性损失，以解决该失败模式。在两个概念验证实验中（语言模型密码游戏和强化学习网格世界），微调显著降低了保留集的预测误差，同时保持了主要任务能力，并实现了与监督基线相当的安全性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of predicting real-world failure rates of ML models when evaluation sets are too small to capture critical failure modes, Jones et al. (2025) propose extrapolating from the top k failure scores to estimate deployment-scale failure rates. They derive a finite-k decomposition of the estimator’s error, revealing a built-in bias toward over-prediction, which is safety-favorable, but can reverse if rare high-failure modes in deployment are absent from the evaluation set. To correct this, they introduce a forecastability loss objective during fine-tuning, which reduces held-out forecast error significantly in two experiments—a language model password game and an RL gridworld—while preserving task performance and achieving safety comparable to supervised baselines.
为了解决评估集规模不足导致难以预测模型部署后真实失败率的问题，Jones 等人（2025）提出从评估集中最高 k 个失败分数进行外推以估计部署规模失败率。他们推导出有限 k 分解公式，表明该估计器通常存在向上偏倚——这是安全有利的方向——除非评估集遗漏了部署中实际存在的罕见高失败模式，此时会导致低估。为此，他们提出在微调中引入预测损失目标，显著降低验证集预测误差，同时保持任务性能，并在两个实验中实现与监督基线相当的安全性。
Causal Foundation Models with Continuous Treatments
Authors: Christopher Stith, Medha Barath, Vahid Balazadeh, Jesse C. Cresswell, Rahul G. Krishnan
First: 2026-05-14T17:40:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:40:28+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Causal inference, estimating causal effects from observational data, is a fundamental tool in many disciplines. Of particular importance across a variety of domains is the continuous treatment setting, where the variable of intervention has a continuous range. This setting is far less explored and represents a substantial shift from the binary treatment setting, with models needing to represent effects across a continuum of treatment values. In this paper, we present the first causal foundation model for the continuous treatment setting. Our model meta-learns the ability to predict causal effects across a wide variety of unseen tasks without additional training or fine-tuning. First, we design a novel prior over data-generating processes with continuous treatment variables in order to generate a rich causal training corpus. We then train a transformer to reconstruct individual treatment-response curves given only observational data, leveraging in-context learning to amortize expensive Bayesian posterior inference. Our model achieves state-of-the-art performance on individual treatment-response curve reconstruction tasks compared to causal models which are trained specifically for those tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：连续处理的因果基础模型
因果推断，即从观测数据中估计因果效应，是许多学科的基本工具。在多个领域中，连续处理设置尤为重要，其中干预变量具有连续取值范围。这一设置远未被充分探索，与二元处理设置相比，模型需要表示在连续处理值范围内的效应。本文提出了首个针对连续处理设置的因果基础模型。我们的模型通过元学习，无需额外训练或微调，即可预测各类未见过任务的因果效应。首先，我们设计了一种针对连续处理变量的数据生成过程的新先验分布，以生成丰富的因果训练语料。接着，我们训练一个Transformer模型，仅根据观测数据重建个体处理-响应曲线，并利用上下文学习来降低昂贵的贝叶斯后验推断成本。与专门为这些任务训练的因果模型相比，我们的模型在个体处理-响应曲线重建任务上达到了最先进性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable causal inference methods in continuous treatment settings—less explored than binary cases—this paper introduces the first causal foundation model designed to estimate causal effects across a continuum of treatment values. The method constructs a novel prior over data-generating processes to generate diverse causal training data, then trains a transformer to reconstruct individual treatment-response curves from observational data using in-context learning to approximate Bayesian inference. Experimental results show the model outperforms task-specific causal models on curve reconstruction tasks, demonstrating strong generalization without fine-tuning.
为应对连续处理场景下因果推断的稀缺性，本文首次提出面向连续处理的因果基础模型。该方法通过构建连续处理变量的数据生成过程先验，生成多样化的因果训练数据，再训练Transformer模型仅依据观测数据重建个体处理-响应曲线，利用上下文学习近似贝叶斯推断。实验表明，该模型在曲线重建任务上达到当前最优性能，无需针对具体任务微调，超越了专门训练的因果模型。
APWA: A Distributed Architecture for Parallelizable Agentic Workflows
Authors: Evan Rose, Tushin Mallick, Matthew D. Laws, Cristina Nita-Rotaru, Alina Oprea
First: 2026-05-14T17:40:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:40:20+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, 2 figures, 14 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous multi-agent systems based on large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable abilities in independently solving complex tasks in a wide breadth of application domains. However, these systems hit critical reasoning, coordination, and computational scaling bottlenecks as the size and complexity of their tasks grow. These limitations hinder multi-agent systems from achieving high-throughput processing for highly parallelizable tasks, despite the availability of parallel computing and reasoning primitives in the underlying LLMs. We introduce the Agent-Parallel Workload Architecture (APWA), a distributed multi-agent system architecture designed for the efficient processing of heavily parallelizable agentic workloads. APWA facilitates parallel execution by decomposing workflows into non-interfering subproblems that can be processed using independent resources without cross-communication. It supports heterogeneous data and parallel processing patterns, and it accommodates tasks from a wide breadth of domains. In our evaluation, we demonstrate that APWA can dynamically decompose complex queries into parallelizable workflows and scales on larger tasks in settings where prior systems fail completely.
中文标题/摘要
标题：APWA：一种支持并行化代理工作流的分布式架构
基于大语言模型（LLMs）的自主多代理系统已在广泛的应用领域中展现出独立解决复杂任务的卓越能力。然而，随着任务规模和复杂度的增长，这些系统面临关键的推理、协调和计算扩展瓶颈。这些限制阻碍了多代理系统在高度并行化任务中实现高吞吐量处理，尽管底层LLMs具备并行计算和推理原语。我们提出了代理并行工作负载架构（APWA），这是一种专为高效处理高度并行化代理工作负载而设计的分布式多代理系统架构。APWA通过将工作流分解为互不干扰的子问题，使其可利用独立资源并行处理，无需跨通信。它支持异构数据和并行处理模式，适用于广泛领域任务。在评估中，我们证明APWA能够动态将复杂查询分解为可并行化的工作流，并在先前系统完全失效的场景中实现对更大任务的扩展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to overcome reasoning, coordination, and scaling bottlenecks in large language model-based multi-agent systems, APWA introduces a distributed architecture designed to efficiently handle highly parallelizable agentic workflows. It achieves this by decomposing complex tasks into non-interfering subproblems that can be executed in parallel across independent resources without requiring cross-communication. Experimental results show that APWA can dynamically break down complex queries into parallelizable components and scales effectively on larger tasks, outperforming prior systems that fail entirely under similar conditions.
为解决基于大语言模型的多智能体系统在推理、协调和扩展性方面的瓶颈，APWA提出了一种分布式架构，专为高效处理高度并行化的智能体工作流而设计。该架构通过将复杂任务分解为可独立执行且无需通信的子任务，实现并行处理。实验表明，APWA能动态将复杂查询分解为可并行化的工作流，并在更大规模任务上实现有效扩展，显著优于此前在相同条件下完全失效的系统。
Natural Synthesis: Outperforming Reactive Synthesis Tools with Large Reasoning Models
Authors: Frederik Schmitt, Matthias Cosler, Niklas Metzger, Julian Siber, Vladimir Krsmanovic, Mohamed Ghanem, Bernd Finkbeiner
First: 2026-05-14T17:39:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:39:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reactive synthesis, the problem of automatically constructing a hardware circuit from a logical specification, is a long-standing challenge in formal verification. It is elusive for two reasons: It is algorithmically hard, and writing formal specifications by hand is notoriously difficult. In this paper, we tackle both sides of the problem. For the algorithmic side, we present a neuro-symbolic approach to reactive synthesis that couples large reasoning models with model checkers to iteratively repair a synthesized Verilog implementation via sound symbolic feedback. Our approach solves more benchmarks than the best dedicated tools in the annual synthesis competition and extends to constructing parameterized systems, a problem known to be undecidable. On the specification side, we introduce an autoformalization step that shifts the specification task from temporal logic to natural language by introducing a hand-authored dataset of natural-language specifications for evaluation. We demonstrate performance comparable to that of starting from formal specifications, establishing natural synthesis as a viable end-to-end workflow.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自然合成：利用大型推理模型超越反应式合成工具
反应式合成，即从逻辑规范自动构建硬件电路的问题，是形式验证领域长期存在的挑战。其难点在于：算法上困难，且手动编写形式规范极为困难。本文同时解决这两个问题。在算法层面，我们提出一种神经符号方法，将大型推理模型与模型检查器结合，通过可靠的符号反馈迭代修复合成的Verilog实现。该方法在年度合成竞赛中解决的基准测试数量超过最佳专用工具，并可扩展至构建参数化系统——一个已知不可判定的问题。在规范层面，我们引入自动形式化步骤，将规范任务从时序逻辑转向自然语言，通过引入人工编写的自然语言规范数据集进行评估。我们展示了其性能与从形式规范出发相当，确立了自然合成作为可行的端到端工作流程。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the algorithmic difficulty of reactive synthesis and the impracticality of hand-written formal specifications, this paper proposes a neuro-symbolic method that combines large reasoning models with model checkers to iteratively refine Verilog implementations using symbolic feedback. The approach outperforms top dedicated tools in annual synthesis benchmarks and successfully handles parameterized systems, previously considered undecidable. Additionally, it introduces an autoformalization pipeline that translates natural language into formal specifications using a hand-authored dataset, achieving performance comparable to starting from formal inputs, thereby validating natural synthesis as a practical end-to-end workflow.
为应对反应式合成的算法难度和人工编写形式化规范的困难，本文提出一种神经符号方法，结合大模型与模型检查器，通过符号反馈迭代优化Verilog实现。该方法在年度合成竞赛中超越现有专用工具，且能处理传统上不可判定的参数化系统。同时，引入自然语言自动形式化流程，将规范任务从时序逻辑转为自然语言，其性能与从形式规范出发相当，验证了自然合成作为端到端流程的可行性。
Sharing the Load: Autonomous Multi-Rover Cargo Transport
Authors: Alexander Krawciw, Luka Antonyshyn, Sven Lilge, Nicolas Olmedo, Faizan Rehmatullah, Maxime Desjardins-Goulet, Pascal Toupin, Timothy D. Barfoot
First: 2025-10-21T16:14:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:39:02+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 14 figures, submitted to IEEE Transactions on Field Robotics
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A future lunar habitat, as part of the Artemis program, will require a significant amount of logistics infrastructure. Cargo that is transported to the Moon will need to be moved from a landing site to other key locations that may be up to 5 km away. Teach and repeat navigation is well suited to this task as utility rovers will need to repeat these cargo routes many times. One of the most significant challenges involves the modules that will be assembled together to form the habitat. Canada is studying potential Lunar Utility Vehicle (LUV) designs to carry these large payloads between the landing site and the location of the habitat. As the details of the cargo continue to evolve, using two, smaller LUVs to carry cargo together would provide high capacity and mission flexibility. In this paper, we develop and implement a distributed model-predictive controller that allows vehicles to carry cargo that is shared between them. The algorithm is compared to baselines in small-scale before being implemented onboard two 800 kg path-to-flight rovers and field tested carrying a 475 kg cargo between them. A custom cargo coupling decouples the kinematics of each vehicle while fully supporting the cargo's mass. In our field test, the rovers maintain a relative separation error of 9.2 cm and maximum error of 33.4 cm. This multi-vehicle control architecture retains the high-quality path tracking of lidar teach and repeat for each rover. We demonstrate that kinematic freedom of the vehicles allows a single controller to provide mission improvements for other operations as well.
中文标题/摘要
标题：分担负载：自主多车货运
作为阿尔忒弥斯计划的一部分，未来月球栖息地将需要大量物流基础设施。运抵月球的货物需从着陆点运输至可能相距5公里的其他关键地点。教与重复导航非常适合此类任务，因为效用漫游车需多次重复这些货运路线。其中最重大挑战之一是将组装成栖息地的模块。加拿大正在研究潜在的月球效用车（LUV）设计，以在着陆点与栖息地位置之间运输这些大型载荷。随着货物细节不断演变，使用两辆较小的LUV协同运输货物可提供高容量和任务灵活性。本文开发并实现了一种分布式模型预测控制器，使车辆能够共同分担载荷。该算法在小型规模下与基准方法比较后，被部署于两辆800公斤级飞行前路径漫游车上，并在实地测试中协同运输475公斤货物。定制的货物耦合装置在解耦各车运动学的同时，完全支持货物质量。在实地测试中，漫游车保持相对分离误差9.2厘米，最大误差33.4厘米。该多车控制架构保留了每辆漫游车激光雷达教与重复导航的高质量路径跟踪能力。我们证明，车辆的运动学自由度使单一控制器可为其他操作提供任务改进。
Summary / 总结
To address the logistical challenge of transporting heavy cargo across long distances on the Moon, this paper proposes a distributed model-predictive control system enabling two autonomous rovers to share a common payload. Motivated by the need for flexible, high-capacity transport in future lunar habitats, the method decouples the kinematics of each rover while maintaining full support for the shared cargo’s mass. Field tests with two 800 kg rovers carrying a 475 kg load demonstrated precise relative positioning—maintaining a 9.2 cm average separation error and a 33.4 cm maximum error—while preserving the high-accuracy path tracking characteristic of lidar teach-and-repeat navigation. The architecture not only improves cargo transport efficiency but also enhances mission adaptability through kinematic freedom.
为解决月球表面长距离重型货物运输的物流难题，本文提出一种分布式模型预测控制方法，使两台自主火星车可协同分担同一负载并保持精确编队。该方法受未来阿耳忒弥斯计划月球基地建设需求驱动，通过定制化货物耦合装置解耦各车运动学，同时支持负载全部质量。实地测试中，两台800公斤火星车协作运输475公斤货物，相对间距误差达9.2厘米，最大误差33.4厘米，同时保留激光教与重复导航的高精度路径跟踪能力。该架构不仅提升运输效率，还通过运动学自由度扩展，为其他任务提供协同控制优势。
MemEye: A Visual-Centric Evaluation Framework for Multimodal Agent Memory
Authors: Minghao Guo, Qingyue Jiao, Zeru Shi, Yihao Quan, Boxuan Zhang, Danrui Li, Liwei Che, Wujiang Xu, Shilong Liu, Zirui Liu, Mubbasir Kapadia, Vladimir Pavlovic, Jiang Liu, Mengdi Wang, Yiyu Shi, Dimitris N. Metaxas, Ruixiang Tang
First: 2026-05-14T17:37:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:37:52+00:00
Comments: 46 pages, 15 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long-term agent memory is increasingly multimodal, yet existing evaluations rarely test whether agents preserve the visual evidence needed for later reasoning. In prior work, many visually grounded questions can be answered using only captions or textual traces, allowing answers to be inferred without preserving the fine-grained visual evidence. Meanwhile, harder cases that require reasoning over changing visual states are largely absent. Therefore, we introduce MemEye, a framework that evaluates memory capabilities from two dimensions: one measures the granularity of decisive visual evidence (from scene-level to pixel-level evidence), and the other measures how retrieved evidence must be used (from single evidence to evolutionary synthesis). Under this framework, we construct a new benchmark across 8 life-scenario tasks, with ablation-driven validation gates for assessing answerability, shortcut resistance, visual necessity, and reasoning structure. By evaluating 13 memory methods across 4 VLM backbones, we show that current architectures still struggle to preserve fine-grained visual details and reason about state changes over time. Our findings show that long-term multimodal memory depends on evidence routing, temporal tracking, and detail extraction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MemEye：面向多模态代理记忆的视觉中心评估框架
长期代理记忆正日益多模态，但现有评估方法很少检验代理是否保留了后续推理所需的视觉证据。在先前研究中，许多视觉接地问题仅通过字幕或文本轨迹即可回答，允许在不保留细粒度视觉证据的情况下推断答案。同时，需要推理视觉状态变化的困难案例则基本缺失。因此，我们提出MemEye框架，从两个维度评估记忆能力：一是衡量决定性视觉证据的粒度（从场景级到像素级证据）；二是衡量检索到的证据如何被使用（从单一证据到演化合成）。在该框架下，我们构建了一个覆盖8个生活场景任务的新基准，包含基于消融验证的评估门控，用于评估可回答性、抗捷径性、视觉必要性及推理结构。通过对4种视觉语言模型（VLM）后端的13种记忆方法进行评估，我们发现当前架构仍难以保留细粒度视觉细节并推理随时间变化的状态。我们的研究结果表明，长期多模态记忆依赖于证据路由、时间追踪与细节提取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap in evaluating whether multimodal agents preserve critical visual evidence for long-term reasoning, MemEye introduces a visual-centric framework that assesses memory through two axes: evidence granularity (from scene to pixel level) and usage mode (from single evidence to evolutionary synthesis). The framework supports a new benchmark across eight life-scenario tasks, validated via ablation gates for answerability, shortcut resistance, visual necessity, and reasoning structure. Evaluating 13 memory methods on four VLM backbones, the study reveals that current architectures fail to retain fine-grained visual details and struggle to reason over temporal visual changes, highlighting the need for evidence routing, temporal tracking, and detail extraction in multimodal memory systems.
为弥补现有方法对多模态智能体是否保留关键视觉证据用于长期推理的评估不足，MemEye提出一个以视觉为中心的评估框架，从两个维度衡量记忆能力：证据粒度（从场景级到像素级）和使用方式（从单一证据到演化合成）。该框架在8个生活场景任务上构建新基准，并通过消融验证门评估答案可得性、捷径抵抗性、视觉必要性及推理结构。实验表明，当前架构普遍难以保留细粒度视觉细节和追踪视觉状态随时间变化，说明有效长期多模态记忆需依赖证据路由、时序追踪与细节提取。
Understanding How International Students in the U.S. Are Using Conversational AI to Support Cross-Cultural Adaptation
Authors: Laleh Nourian, Anisa Callis, Stephanie Patterson, Jadeline Miao, Jamison Heard, Garreth W. Tigwell
First: 2026-05-14T17:37:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:37:23+00:00
Comments: 33 pages, single column. 4 figures, 9 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Moving to a new culture and adapting to a new life, as an international student, can be a stressful experience. In the US, international students face unique overlapping challenges, yet the current support ecosystem, including university support systems and informal social networks, remains largely fragmented. While conversational AI has emerged as a tool used by many (e.g., generative AI chatbots like ChatGPT and Google Gemini), we do not have a clear understanding of how international students adopt and perceive these technologies as support tools. We conducted a survey study (n=60) to map the relationship between international students' challenges and AI adoption patterns, followed by an interview study with 14 participants to identify the underlying motivations and boundaries of use. Our findings show that AI is perceived as a first-aid tool for immediate challenges, however, there is an interest in transforming AI from a tool for short-term help into a long-term support companion. By identifying where and how AI can provide long-term support, and where it is insufficient, we contribute recommendations for creating AI-powered support tailored to the unique needs of international students.
中文标题/摘要
标题：理解美国国际学生如何利用对话式人工智能支持跨文化适应
作为国际学生移居新文化并适应新生活，可能是一种压力重重的经历。在美国，国际学生面临独特的多重挑战，但当前的支持体系——包括大学支持系统和非正式社交网络——仍较为分散。尽管对话式人工智能（如ChatGPT和Google Gemini等生成式AI聊天机器人）已成为许多人使用的工具，但我们对国际学生如何采纳并感知这些技术作为支持工具仍缺乏清晰理解。我们通过一项调查研究（n=60）绘制了国际学生挑战与AI采纳模式之间的关系，随后对14名参与者进行访谈，以识别其使用动机与边界。研究发现，AI被视为应对即时挑战的急救工具，但国际学生希望将其从短期帮助工具转变为长期支持伙伴。通过识别AI在何处及如何提供长期支持、以及其不足之处，我们为针对国际学生独特需求定制AI支持系统提出建议。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the fragmented support systems international students face in the U.S., this study explores how they use conversational AI to navigate cross-cultural adaptation. Through a survey of 60 students and interviews with 14, researchers mapped AI adoption patterns against specific challenges, revealing that students primarily view AI as a first-aid tool for immediate stressors. However, participants expressed a desire to evolve AI into a long-term companion for ongoing adaptation. The study identifies key contexts where AI can provide sustained support and where it falls short, offering actionable insights for designing more effective, student-centered AI support systems.
鉴于国际学生在美国面临支持体系碎片化的问题，本研究探讨了他们如何利用对话式AI辅助跨文化适应。通过对60名学生的问卷调查和14名学生的深度访谈，研究人员梳理了AI使用模式与学生挑战之间的关联，并识别了使用动机。研究发现，学生将AI主要视为应对即时压力的急救工具，但同时希望将其发展为长期陪伴式支持伙伴，指出了AI当前不足之处，并为构建针对国际学生独特需求的AI支持系统提供了方向。
Usable but Conventional: An Empirical Study on the UX of AI-Generated Interface Prototypes
Authors: Karoline Romero, Igor Wiese, Renato Balancieiri, Gislaine Camila Leal, Guilherme Guerino
First: 2026-05-14T17:36:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:36:57+00:00
Comments: Paper accepted for presentation at SEMISH 2026 - 53rd Integrated Software and Hardware Seminar (Congress of the Brazilian Computer Society - 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper investigates User Experience (UX) with prototypes generated by Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) tools. An empirical survey with 92 participants evaluated AI-generated and human-created prototypes without prior identification of authorship. We measured UX using the UEQ-S, covering pragmatic and hedonic dimensions. Results indicate positive evaluations in pragmatic aspects, such as usability and efficiency, and neutral or negative evaluations in hedonic aspects, including originality and innovation. We concluded that GenAI can produce functional interfaces but tends to reinforce visual and structural patterns that affect perceptions of originality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：可用但常规：关于AI生成界面原型用户体验的实证研究
本文研究了由生成式人工智能（GenAI）工具生成的原型的用户体验（UX）。一项针对92名参与者的实证调查评估了AI生成与人工创建的原型，且未提前标识作者身份。我们使用UEQ-S量表测量UX，涵盖实用性和享乐性维度。结果显示，实用方面（如可用性与效率）评价积极，而享乐性方面（如原创性与创新性）评价中性或消极。我们得出结论：GenAI可生成功能性的界面，但倾向于强化视觉与结构模式，影响原创性感知。
Summary / 总结
This study explores user experience with AI-generated interface prototypes, motivated by the growing use of Generative AI tools in design. Using a survey of 92 participants who evaluated both AI- and human-made prototypes without knowing their origin, the researchers measured UX via the UEQ-S scale across pragmatic and hedonic dimensions. Results showed positive ratings for usability and efficiency but neutral or negative scores for originality and innovation, suggesting that while GenAI produces functional interfaces, it often reinforces conventional visual and structural patterns that reduce perceived novelty.
鉴于生成式人工智能在界面设计中的广泛应用，本研究通过92名参与者对AI生成与人工设计的原型进行盲测，评估其用户体验。使用UEQ-S量表测量实用性（如易用性、效率）与享乐性（如原创性、创新性）维度。结果表明，AI生成原型在实用性方面获得正面评价，但在享乐性方面评价中性或负面，说明AI生成界面虽功能可用，但常复制传统视觉与结构模式，影响用户对原创性的感知。
Why Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning Works: Relation to Dual Control
Authors: Nathan P. Lawrence, Ali Mesbah
First: 2025-12-06T15:28:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:36:34+00:00
Comments: IFAC world congress postprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (RL) concerns the problem of training an agent to maximize the probability of reaching target goal states. This paper presents an analysis of the goal-conditioned setting based on optimal control. In particular, we derive an optimality gap between more classical, often quadratic, objectives and the goal-conditioned reward, elucidating the success of goal-conditioned RL and why classical ``dense'' rewards can falter. We then consider the partially observed Markov decision setting and connect state estimation to our probabilistic reward, making the goal-conditioned reward well suited to dual control problems. The advantages of goal-conditioned policies are validated on nonlinear and uncertain environments using both RL and predictive control techniques.
中文标题/摘要
标题：目标条件强化学习为何有效：与双重控制的关系
目标条件强化学习（RL）关注训练智能体以最大化到达目标状态的概率。本文基于最优控制理论，对目标条件设置进行了分析。特别是，我们推导了经典（常为二次型）目标函数与目标条件奖励之间的最优性差距，阐明了目标条件RL的成功原因，以及为何经典“密集”奖励可能失效。随后，我们考虑部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程，并将状态估计与概率奖励相联系，使目标条件奖励适用于双重控制问题。通过强化学习和预测控制技术，在非线性和不确定环境中验证了目标条件策略的优势。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates why goal-conditioned reinforcement learning succeeds by linking it to principles of dual control through an optimal control framework. It derives an optimality gap between classical quadratic objectives and goal-conditioned rewards, explaining why dense rewards often underperform in such settings. The authors extend the analysis to partially observed environments, showing how state estimation naturally integrates with the probabilistic structure of goal-conditioned rewards, making them ideal for dual control. Experimental validation across nonlinear and uncertain systems using both RL and predictive control confirms the effectiveness of goal-conditioned policies.
本文从最优控制视角分析了目标条件强化学习为何有效，推导出目标奖励与经典二次奖励之间的最优性差距，解释了目标奖励在收敛性和鲁棒性上的优势。作者进一步将该框架扩展至部分可观测环境，将状态估计与目标奖励的概率特性关联，揭示其与双控制问题的契合性。在非线性和不确定环境中，通过强化学习与预测控制方法的实验验证，目标条件策略在性能上显著优于传统奖励形式。
SyncLight: Single-Edit Multi-View Relighting
Authors: David Serrano-Lozano, Anand Bhattad, Luis Herranz, Jean-François Lalonde, Javier Vazquez-Corral
First: 2026-01-23T18:59:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:35:56+00:00
Comments: Project page: http://sync-light.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We present SyncLight, a method to enable consistent, parametric control over light sources across multiple uncalibrated views of a static scene conditioned on a single view. While single-view relighting has advanced significantly, existing generative approaches struggle to maintain the rigorous lighting consistency essential for multi-camera broadcasts, stereoscopic cinema, and virtual production. SyncLight addresses this by enabling precise control over light intensity and color across a multi-view capture of a scene, conditioned on a single reference edit. Our method leverages a multi-view diffusion transformer trained using a latent bridge matching formulation, achieving high-fidelity relighting of the entire image set in a single inference step. To facilitate training, we introduce a large-scale hybrid dataset comprising diverse synthetic environments -- curated from existing sources and newly designed scenes -- alongside high-fidelity, real-world multi-view captures under calibrated illumination. Though trained only on image pairs, SyncLight generalizes zero-shot to an arbitrary number of viewpoints, effectively propagating lighting changes across all views, without requiring camera pose information. SyncLight enables practical relighting workflows for multi-view capture systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SyncLight：单次编辑多视角重照明
我们提出了SyncLight，一种方法，可在多个未校准视角下，基于单视角条件，实现对静态场景光源的一致性、参数化控制。尽管单视角重照明已取得显著进展，但现有生成方法难以维持多摄像机广播、立体电影和虚拟制作所必需的严格光照一致性。SyncLight通过基于单参考编辑实现对场景多视角捕获中光照强度与颜色的精确控制，解决了这一问题。该方法利用一种多视角扩散Transformer，采用潜在桥匹配形式训练，可在单次推理步骤中实现整组图像的高保真重照明。为促进训练，我们构建了一个大规模混合数据集，包含多样化的合成环境——部分来自现有资源，部分为新设计场景——以及在标定光照下拍摄的高保真真实多视角图像。尽管仅在图像对上训练，SyncLight可零样本泛化至任意数量视角，无需相机姿态信息，即可有效将光照变化传播至所有视角。SyncLight为多视角捕获系统提供了实用的重照明工作流程。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for lighting consistency across multiple uncalibrated views in applications like virtual production and stereoscopic cinema, SyncLight enables parametric control of light sources across all views using only a single edited reference view. It employs a multi-view diffusion transformer trained via latent bridge matching to propagate lighting edits from one view to others, achieving high-fidelity relighting in a single inference step. Evaluated on a large-scale hybrid dataset combining synthetic and real multi-view captures, SyncLight generalizes zero-shot to any number of viewpoints without needing camera poses, demonstrating robust performance across diverse scenes and lighting conditions.
为满足虚拟制作和立体电影等场景中多视角光照一致性需求，SyncLight提出一种仅依赖单视角编辑即可控制多视角光照参数的方法。该方法采用基于潜在桥匹配训练的多视角扩散Transformer，在单次推理中即可实现整组多视角图像的高保真重光照。在融合合成与真实多视角数据的大型混合数据集上验证，SyncLight无需相机位姿信息，即可零样本泛化至任意视角，有效传播光照修改至所有视图，同时保持视觉保真度。
CoCo-InEKF: State Estimation with Learned Contact Covariances in Dynamic, Contact-Rich Scenarios
Authors: Michael Baumgartner, David Müller, Agon Serifi, Ruben Grandia, Espen Knoop, Markus Gross, Moritz Bächer
Venue: RSS 2026
First: 2026-05-14T17:35:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:35:06+00:00
Comments: RSS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robust state estimation for highly dynamic motion of legged robots remains challenging, especially in dynamic, contact-rich scenarios. Traditional approaches often rely on binary contact states that fail to capture the nuances of partial contact or directional slippage. This paper presents CoCo-InEKF, a differentiable invariant extended Kalman filter that utilizes continuous contact velocity covariances instead of binary contact states. These learned covariances allow the method to dynamically modulate contact confidence, accounting for more nuanced conditions ranging from firm contact to directional slippage or no contact. To predict these covariances for a set of predefined contact candidate points, we employ a lightweight neural network trained end-to-end using a state-error loss. This approach eliminates the need for heuristic ground-truth contact labels. In addition, we propose an automated contact candidate selection procedure and demonstrate that our method is insensitive to their exact placement. Experiments on a bipedal robot demonstrate a superior accuracy-efficiency tradeoff for linear velocity estimation, as well as improved filter consistency compared to baseline methods. This enables the robust execution of challenging motions, including dancing and complex ground interactions -- both in simulation and in the real world.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CoCo-InEKF：在动态接触丰富的场景中利用学习到的接触协方差进行状态估计
腿式机器人高度动态运动的状态估计仍具挑战性，尤其是在动态接触丰富的场景中。传统方法常依赖二元接触状态，无法捕捉部分接触或方向性滑移的细微差别。本文提出CoCo-InEKF，一种可微分的不变扩展卡尔曼滤波器，使用连续接触速度协方差替代二元接触状态。这些学习到的协方差使方法能够动态调节接触置信度，涵盖从牢固接触、方向性滑移到无接触等更细微的条件。为预测预定义接触候选点集的协方差，我们采用轻量级神经网络，通过状态误差损失端到端训练，无需人工标注的接触真值。此外，我们提出自动化接触候选点选择流程，并证明方法对候选点精确位置不敏感。在双足机器人实验中，该方法在线速度估计上展现出更优的精度-效率权衡，且相比基线方法滤波一致性更优，从而支持在仿真与现实世界中执行复杂动作，如舞蹈和复杂地面交互。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of binary contact models in accurately capturing partial contact or slippage during legged robot motion, this work introduces CoCo-InEKF, a differentiable invariant EKF that replaces binary contact states with learned continuous contact velocity covariances. These covariances are predicted by a lightweight neural network trained end-to-end using state-error loss, eliminating the need for ground-truth contact labels. The method also includes an automated contact candidate selection scheme that is robust to placement variations. Experimental results on a bipedal robot show CoCo-InEKF achieves superior accuracy-efficiency balance in linear velocity estimation and improved filter consistency compared to baselines, enabling robust execution of complex motions like dancing and dynamic ground interactions in both simulation and real-world settings.
为解决传统二值接触模型在动态足式机器人运动中的不足，本文提出CoCo-InEKF方法，采用可微分不变扩展卡尔曼滤波，用连续的、学习得到的接触速度协方差替代离散接触状态。这些协方差由轻量级神经网络端到端训练，以状态误差损失为目标，无需人工标注接触标签，即可适应从紧密接触、滑移到无接触等多种接触状态。实验表明，该方法在直线速度估计上实现更优的精度-效率平衡，且滤波一致性优于基线方法，成功支持机器人在仿真与真实环境中完成舞蹈、复杂地面交互等高难度动作。
Proxy Compression for Language Modeling
Authors: Lin Zheng, Xinyu Li, Qian Liu, Xiachong Feng, Lingpeng Kong
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-04T07:36:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:33:56+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Modern language models are trained almost exclusively on token sequences produced by a fixed tokenizer, an external lossless compressor often over UTF-8 byte sequences, thereby coupling the model to that compressor. This work introduces proxy compression, an alternative training scheme that preserves the efficiency benefits of compressed inputs while providing an end-to-end, raw-byte interface at inference time. During training, a single language model is jointly trained on raw byte sequences and compressed views generated by external compressors; through the process, the model learns to internally align compressed sequences and raw bytes. This alignment enables strong transfer between the two formats, even when training predominantly on compressed inputs that are discarded at inference. Extensive experiments on code language modeling demonstrate that proxy compression substantially improves training efficiency and significantly outperforms pure byte-level baselines given fixed compute budgets. As model scale increases, these gains become more pronounced, and proxy-trained models eventually match or surpass tokenizer approaches, all while operating solely on raw bytes and retaining the inherent robustness of byte-level modeling. Our code is available at https://github.com/LZhengisme/proxy-compression.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言建模中的代理压缩
现代语言模型几乎完全依赖固定分词器生成的标记序列进行训练，而该分词器通常基于UTF-8字节序列的外部无损压缩器，从而将模型与该压缩器耦合。本文提出代理压缩，这是一种替代训练方案，它在保持压缩输入效率优势的同时，为推理阶段提供端到端的原始字节接口。训练过程中，单一语言模型同时在原始字节序列和由外部压缩器生成的压缩视图上联合训练；通过该过程，模型学习在内部对齐压缩序列与原始字节。这种对齐使得两种格式间实现强迁移，即使训练主要基于在推理阶段被丢弃的压缩输入。在代码语言建模上的大量实验表明，代理压缩显著提升训练效率，并在计算预算固定的情况下显著优于纯字节级基线。随着模型规模增大，这些收益愈发明显，代理训练模型最终可匹配或超越分词器方法，且全程仅使用原始字节，同时保留字节级建模的固有鲁棒性。代码见 https://github.com/LZhengisme/proxy-compression。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of training language models on tokenized inputs that are tightly coupled to specific tokenizers and compressors, this work proposes proxy compression: a training method that jointly trains a model on raw byte sequences and compressed views from external compressors, enabling the model to learn internal alignments between the two formats. During inference, the model operates on raw bytes without needing the compressor, preserving efficiency while maintaining robustness. Experiments on code language modeling show that proxy compression significantly improves training efficiency and outperforms pure byte-level baselines under fixed compute budgets, with performance gains increasing as model scale grows, eventually matching or surpassing tokenizer-based approaches.
为了解决语言模型训练中因依赖固定分词器和压缩器而导致的效率问题，该研究提出代理压缩方法：在训练阶段，模型同时学习原始字节序列与外部压缩器生成的压缩视图，从而在内部建立两者间的对齐关系。推理时模型直接处理原始字节，无需压缩器，既保持效率又保留字节级建模的鲁棒性。在代码建模实验中，代理压缩显著提升训练效率，且在固定算力下明显优于纯字节级基线，随着模型规模增大，性能优势更显著，最终可媲美甚至超越传统分词器方法。
Hyperbolic Graph Neural Networks Under the Microscope: The Role of Geometry-Task Alignment
Authors: Dionisia Naddeo, Jonas Linkerhägner, Nicola Toschi, Geri Skenderi, Veronica Lachi
First: 2026-02-02T09:01:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:33:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many complex networks exhibit hierarchical, tree-like structures, making hyperbolic space a natural candidate wherein to learn representations of them. Based on this observation, Hyperbolic Graph Neural Networks (HGNNs) have been widely adopted as a principled choice for representation learning on tree-like graphs. In this work, we question this paradigm by proposing the additional condition of geometry--task alignment, i.e., whether the metric structure of the target follows that of the input graph. We theoretically and empirically demonstrate the capability of HGNNs to recover low-distortion representations on regression problems, and show that their geometric inductive bias becomes helpful when the problem requires preserving metric structure. By jointly analyzing predictive performance and embedding distortion, we further show that HGNNs gain an advantage on link prediction, a naturally geometry-aligned task, whereas this advantage largely disappears on standard node classification benchmarks, which are typically not geometry--aligned. Overall, our findings shift the focus from only asking "Is the graph hyperbolic?" to also questioning "Is the task aligned with hyperbolic geometry?", showing that HGNNs consistently outperform Euclidean models under such alignment, while their advantage vanishes otherwise.
中文标题/摘要
标题：显微镜下的双曲图神经网络：几何与任务对齐的作用
许多复杂网络表现出层次化、树状结构，使得双曲空间成为学习其表示的自然候选。基于这一观察，双曲图神经网络（HGNNs）被广泛采用作为树状图表示学习的合理选择。本文通过提出几何-任务对齐的附加条件，质疑这一范式，即目标的度量结构是否与输入图的结构一致。我们从理论和实证上证明了HGNNs在回归问题中能够恢复低失真表示，并表明当任务需要保持度量结构时，其几何归纳偏置变得有益。通过联合分析预测性能和嵌入失真，我们进一步表明，HGNNs在链接预测任务（天然几何对齐）上具有优势，而在标准节点分类基准（通常非几何对齐）上，这一优势基本消失。总体而言，我们的发现将关注点从仅问“图是否是双曲的？”扩展到也问“任务是否与双曲几何对齐？”，表明在对齐条件下HGNNs始终优于欧氏模型，否则其优势消失。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that many real-world networks exhibit tree-like, hierarchical structures, this work investigates whether Hyperbolic Graph Neural Networks (HGNNs) are inherently suited for all graph tasks or only when the task aligns with hyperbolic geometry. The authors propose the concept of geometry-task alignment, empirically and theoretically demonstrating that HGNNs excel in tasks requiring preservation of metric structure, such as link prediction, where their geometric bias provides a consistent advantage over Euclidean models. However, on standard node classification benchmarks, which typically do not align with hyperbolic geometry, HGNNs show little to no advantage over Euclidean GNNs, suggesting their performance is contingent on task-geometry compatibility.
鉴于许多现实网络具有树状、分层结构，本文探讨了超球面几何是否天然适用于图表示学习。提出“几何-任务对齐”概念，质疑任务的度量需求是否与输入图的超球面结构一致。理论分析与实验表明，超球面图神经网络（HGNN）在需要保持度量结构的任务（如链接预测）中能有效保持低失真嵌入，但在通常不与超球面结构对齐的标准节点分类任务中优势不明显。结果表明，HGNN的性能优势取决于任务与几何结构的对齐程度，而非仅由图拓扑决定。
CLOVER: Closed-Loop Value Estimation \& Ranking for End-to-End Autonomous Driving Planning
Authors: Sining Ang, Yuguang Yang, Canyu Chen, Yan Wang
First: 2026-05-14T17:32:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:32:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
End-to-end autonomous driving planners are commonly trained by imitating a single logged trajectory, yet evaluated by rule-based planning metrics that measure safety, feasibility, progress, and comfort. This creates a training--evaluation mismatch: trajectories close to the logged path may violate planning rules, while alternatives farther from the demonstration can remain valid and high-scoring. The mismatch is especially limiting for proposal-selection planners, whose performance depends on candidate-set coverage and scorer ranking quality. We propose CLOVER, a Closed-LOop Value Estimation and Ranking framework for end-to-end autonomous driving planning. CLOVER follows a lightweight generator--scorer formulation: a generator produces diverse candidate trajectories, and a scorer predicts planning-metric sub-scores to rank them at inference time. To expand proposal support beyond single-trajectory imitation, CLOVER constructs evaluator-filtered pseudo-expert trajectories and trains the generator with set-level coverage supervision. It then performs conservative closed-loop self-distillation: the scorer is fitted to true evaluator sub-scores on generated proposals, while the generator is refined toward teacher-selected top-$k$ and vector-Pareto targets with stability regularization. We analyze when an imperfect scorer can improve the generator, showing that scorer-mediated refinement is reliable when scorer-selected targets are enriched under the true evaluator and updates remain conservative. On NAVSIM, CLOVER achieves 94.5 PDMS and 90.4 EPDMS, establishing a new state of the art. On the more challenging NavHard split, it obtains 48.3 EPDMS, matching the strongest reported result. On supplementary nuScenes open-loop evaluation, CLOVER achieves the lowest L2 error and collision rate among compared methods. Code data will be released at https://github.com/WilliamXuanYu/CLOVER.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CLOVER：面向端到端自动驾驶规划的闭环价值评估与排序
端到端自动驾驶规划器通常通过模仿单一记录轨迹进行训练，但评估时却使用基于规则的规划指标，衡量安全性、可行性、进度和舒适性。这导致训练与评估之间的不匹配：接近记录路径的轨迹可能违反规划规则，而偏离演示轨迹的替代方案可能仍有效且得分高。这种不匹配对提案选择型规划器尤为不利，其性能依赖于候选集覆盖度和评分器排序质量。我们提出CLOVER，一种面向端到端自动驾驶规划的闭环价值评估与排序框架。CLOVER采用轻量级生成器-评分器结构：生成器产生多样化的候选轨迹，评分器预测规划指标子分数并在推理时对其进行排序。为扩展单轨迹模仿之外的提案支持，CLOVER构建评估器过滤的伪专家轨迹，并以集合级覆盖监督训练生成器。随后，CLOVER执行保守的闭环自蒸馏：评分器在生成提案上拟合真实评估器子分数，而生成器则朝向教师选择的前k个及向量帕累托目标进行优化，并施加稳定性正则化。我们分析了不完美评分器如何提升生成器性能，表明当评分器选择的目标在真实评估器下得到丰富且更新保持保守时，评分器介导的优化是可靠的。在NAVSIM上，CLOVER实现94.5 PDMS和90.4 EPDMS，创下新纪录。在更具挑战性的NavHard子集上，其获得48.3 EPDMS，与当前最佳结果持平。在补充的nuScenes开环评估中，CLOVER在对比方法中实现最低L2误差和碰撞率。代码与数据将在https://github.com/WilliamXuanYu/CLOVER发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the mismatch between training via single-trajectory imitation and evaluation using rule-based metrics, CLOVER introduces a closed-loop framework for end-to-end autonomous driving planning. It employs a lightweight generator-scoring architecture where a generator produces diverse candidate trajectories and a scorer predicts sub-scores to rank them at inference. To improve proposal coverage, the generator is trained with set-level supervision using evaluator-filtered pseudo-expert trajectories. The scorer is refined via self-distillation using true evaluator scores, while the generator is updated toward teacher-selected top-k and Pareto-optimal targets with stability regularization. Experiments on NAVSIM show CLOVER achieves 94.5 PDMS and 90.4 EPDMS, setting a new state-of-the-art, and on NavHard it matches the best reported EPDMS score of 48.3. On nuScenes, it also achieves the lowest L2 error and collision rate among compared methods.
为解决端到端自动驾驶规划中训练（单轨迹模仿）与评估（规则基指标）不一致的问题，CLOVER提出一个闭环价值估计与排序框架，联合优化轨迹生成器与评分器。生成器产生多样轨迹，评分器预测子分数用于推理时排序。为扩展提案覆盖范围，生成器以评估器过滤的伪专家轨迹为监督进行训练。评分器通过自蒸馏使用真实评估器分数进行优化，生成器则向教师选择的Top-k和向量帕累托目标优化，辅以稳定性正则化。在NAVSIM上，CLOVER达到94.5 PDMS和90.4 EPDMS，刷新当前最优；在更具挑战性的NavHard上，其EPDMS达48.3，匹配现有最佳结果；在nuScenes开放环评估中，其L2误差与碰撞率均为最低。
RoboLab: A High-Fidelity Simulation Benchmark for Analysis of Task Generalist Policies
Authors: Xuning Yang, Rishit Dagli, Alex Zook, Hugo Hadfield, Ankit Goyal, Stan Birchfield, Fabio Ramos, Jonathan Tremblay
Venue: Robotics: Science and Systems XXII, Sydney, Australia, 2026
First: 2026-04-10T19:42:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:31:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
The pursuit of general-purpose robotics has yielded impressive foundation models, yet simulation-based benchmarking remains a bottleneck due to rapid performance saturation and a lack of true generalization testing. Existing benchmarks often exhibit significant domain overlap between training and evaluation, trivializing success rates and obscuring insights into robustness. We introduce RoboLab, a simulation benchmarking framework designed to address these challenges. Concretely, our framework is designed to answer two questions: (1) to what extent can we understand the performance of a real-world policy by analyzing its behavior in simulation, and (2) which factor most strongly affect policy behavior. First, RoboLab enables human-authored and LLM-enabled generation of scenes and tasks in a robot- and policy-agnostic manner within a high-fidelity simulation environment. We introduce an accompanying RoboLab-120 benchmark, consisting of 120 tasks categorized into three competency axes: visual, procedural, relational, across three difficulty levels. Second, we introduce a systematic analysis of real-world policies that quantify both their performance and the sensitivity of their behavior to controlled perturbations, exposing significant performance gap in current state-of-the-art models. By providing granular metrics and a scalable toolset, RoboLab offers a scalable framework for evaluating the true generalization capabilities of task-generalist robotic policies. Project website: https://research.nvidia.com/labs/srl/projects/robolab/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RoboLab：用于分析任务通用策略的高保真仿真基准
通用机器人研究已取得令人瞩目的基础模型成果，但基于仿真的基准测试仍受限于性能快速饱和及缺乏真实泛化能力测试。现有基准常存在训练与评估域高度重叠，导致成功率虚高且掩盖鲁棒性洞察。我们提出RoboLab仿真基准框架以应对这些挑战。具体而言，该框架旨在回答两个问题：(1) 通过分析策略在仿真中的表现，我们能多大程度理解其真实世界性能？(2) 哪一因素最显著影响策略行为？首先，RoboLab支持人类与大语言模型在高保真仿真环境中以机器人与策略无关的方式生成场景与任务。我们配套推出RoboLab-120基准，包含120项任务，按视觉、程序、关系三大能力轴划分，涵盖三个难度等级。其次，我们系统分析真实世界策略，量化其性能及对受控扰动的敏感性，揭示当前最先进模型存在显著性能差距。通过提供细粒度指标与可扩展工具集，RoboLab为评估任务通用型机器人策略的真实泛化能力提供可扩展框架。项目网站：https://research.nvidia.com/labs/srl/projects/robolab/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate task-generalist robotic policies beyond superficial simulation performance, RoboLab introduces a high-fidelity simulation benchmark designed to probe true generalization. It enables human and LLM-driven generation of diverse, policy-agnostic tasks across visual, procedural, and relational competency axes, with 120 tasks spanning three difficulty levels. Experimental results reveal that current state-of-the-art policies exhibit significant performance gaps when subjected to controlled perturbations, indicating limited robustness and generalization. RoboLab’s granular metrics and scalable analysis tools expose these weaknesses, offering a more rigorous framework for assessing real-world policy behavior in simulation.
为突破现有仿真评估对机器人通用策略真实泛化能力的局限，RoboLab构建了一个高保真仿真基准，支持人类与大模型生成与机器人和策略无关的任务与场景。该基准包含120个任务，按视觉、程序和关系三大能力轴划分，分三个难度层级评估。实验表明，当前最先进的通用策略在受控扰动下表现显著下降，揭示其鲁棒性不足，而传统基准因训练与评估域高度重叠，导致评估结果失真。RoboLab通过细粒度指标与可扩展工具，为真实世界策略的泛化能力评估提供了更可靠的框架。
Talk is (Not) Cheap: A Taxonomy and Benchmark Coverage Audit for LLM Attacks
Authors: Karthik Raghu Iyer, Yazdan Jamshidi, Nicholas Bray, Alexey A. Shvets
First: 2026-05-14T17:30:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:30:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce a reusable framework for auditing whether LLM attack benchmarks collectively cover the threat surface: a 4$\times$6 Target $\times$ Technique matrix grounded in STRIDE, constructed from a 507-leaf taxonomy -- 401 data-populated and 106 threat-model-derived leaves -- of inference-time attacks extracted from 932 arXiv security studies (2023--2026). The matrix enables benchmark-external validation -- auditing collective coverage rather than individual benchmark consistency. Applying it to six public benchmarks reveals that the three primary frameworks (HarmBench, InjecAgent, AgentDojo) occupy non-overlapping cells covering at most 25\% of the matrix, while entire STRIDE threat categories (Service Disruption, Model Internals) lack any standardized evaluation, despite published attacks in these categories achieving 46$\times$ token amplification and 96\% attack success rates through mechanisms which no benchmark tests. The corpus of 2,521 unique attack groups further reveals pervasive naming fragmentation (up to 29 surface forms for a single attack) and heavy concentration in Safety \& Alignment Bypass, structural properties invisible at smaller scale. The taxonomy, attack records, and coverage mappings are released as extensible artifacts; as new benchmarks emerge, they can be mapped onto the same matrix, enabling the community to track whether evaluation gaps are closing.
中文标题/摘要
标题：言辞（并非）廉价：大语言模型攻击的分类与基准覆盖审计
我们提出了一种可复用的框架，用于审计大语言模型攻击基准是否共同覆盖了威胁面：一个基于STRIDE的4×6目标×技术矩阵，由从932篇arXiv安全研究（2023–2026）中提取的507个攻击分类节点构成——其中401个节点由数据填充，106个节点由威胁模型推导。该矩阵支持基准外部验证——审计整体覆盖范围而非单个基准的一致性。将其应用于六个公开基准发现，三大主流框架（HarmBench、InjecAgent、AgentDojo）占据互不重叠的单元格，覆盖矩阵最多25%；而整个STRIDE威胁类别（服务中断、模型内部）缺乏标准化评估，尽管这些类别中已发表的攻击通过未被任何基准测试的机制实现了46倍token放大和96%攻击成功率。2,521个独特攻击组的语料库进一步揭示了普遍的命名碎片化（单一攻击多达29种表面形式）及在安全与对齐绕过方面的高度集中，这些结构性特征在小规模下不可见。该分类体系、攻击记录与覆盖映射作为可扩展的成果发布；随着新基准的出现，可映射至同一矩阵，使社区能够追踪评估缺口是否正在缩小。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess whether large language model (LLM) attack benchmarks collectively cover the full threat landscape, the authors construct a 4×6 Target×Technique matrix based on STRIDE, derived from a 507-leaf taxonomy of inference-time attacks extracted from 932 arXiv security studies. Applied to six public benchmarks, the framework reveals that major benchmarks like HarmBench, InjecAgent, and AgentDojo cover only up to 25% of the matrix with non-overlapping coverage, while critical STRIDE categories such as Service Disruption and Model Internals remain entirely untested despite documented attacks achieving 46× token amplification and 96% success rates. The analysis also uncovers severe naming fragmentation (up to 29 variants for one attack) and an overrepresentation of Safety & Alignment Bypass attacks, highlighting gaps invisible at smaller scales.
为评估现有大语言模型攻击基准是否全面覆盖现实威胁，作者基于STRIDE构建了一个4×6的攻击目标×技术矩阵，该矩阵源自从932篇arXiv安全论文中提取的507个攻击分类。应用该框架评估六个公开基准发现，主流框架如HarmBench、InjecAgent和AgentDojo合计仅覆盖矩阵25%以下，而如服务中断和模型内部等完整STRIDE类别缺乏标准化评估，尽管文献中已记录到这些类别攻击成功率高达96%且可实现46倍token放大。分析还发现攻击命名高度碎片化（单个攻击多达29种名称），且集中于安全与对齐绕过，这些在小规模分析中难以察觉。
DriveCtrl: Conditioned Sim-to-Real Driving Video Generation
Authors: Haonan Zhao, Yiting Wang, Jingkun Chen, Valentina Donzella, Thomas Bashford-Rogers, Kurt Debattista
First: 2026-05-14T17:29:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:29:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large-scale labelled driving video data is essential for training autonomous driving systems. Although simulation offers scalable and fully annotated data, the domain gap between synthetic and real-world driving videos significantly limits its utility for downstream deployment. Existing video generation methods are not well-suited for this task, as they fail to simultaneously preserve scene structure, object dynamics, temporal consistency, and visual realism, all of which are critical for maintaining annotation validity in generated data. In this paper, we present DriveCtrl, a depth-conditioned controllable sim-to-real video generation framework for realistic driving video synthesis. Built upon a pretrained video foundation model, DriveCtrl introduces a structure-aware adapter that enables depth-guided generation while preserving the scene layout and motion patterns of the source simulation, producing temporally coherent driving videos that remain aligned with the original simulated sequences. We further introduce a scalable data generation pipeline that transforms simulator videos into realistic driving footage matching the visual style of a target real-world dataset. The pipeline supports three conditioning signals: structural depth, reference-dataset style, and text prompts, while preserving frame-level annotations for downstream perception tasks. To better assess this task, we propose a driving-domain-specific knowledge-informed evaluation metric called Driving Video Realism Score (DVRS) that assesses the realism of generated videos. Experiments demonstrate that DriveCtrl consistently outperforms the base model and competing alternatives in realism, temporal quality, and perception task performance, substantially narrowing the sim-to-real gap for driving video generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DriveCtrl：基于条件的仿真到真实驾驶视频生成
大规模标注的驾驶视频数据对自动驾驶系统训练至关重要。尽管仿真可提供可扩展且全标注的数据，但合成与真实世界驾驶视频之间的域差距显著限制了其在下游部署中的实用性。现有视频生成方法不适合此任务，因其无法同时保持场景结构、物体动态、时间一致性及视觉真实感，而这些要素对生成数据的标注有效性至关重要。本文提出DriveCtrl，一种基于深度条件的可控仿真到真实视频生成框架，用于合成逼真的驾驶视频。该框架基于预训练视频基础模型，引入结构感知适配器，实现深度引导生成的同时保留源仿真场景布局与运动模式，生成时间上连贯且与原始仿真序列保持一致的驾驶视频。我们进一步提出可扩展的数据生成流水线，将仿真视频转换为与目标真实数据集视觉风格匹配的逼真驾驶画面。该流水线支持三种条件信号：结构深度、参考数据集风格及文本提示，并保留帧级标注以支持下游感知任务。为更好地评估该任务，我们提出一种面向驾驶领域的知识引导评估指标——驾驶视频真实度评分（DVRS），用于评估生成视频的真实性。实验表明，DriveCtrl在真实感、时间质量及感知任务表现上持续优于基线模型和竞争方法，显著缩小了仿真到真实视频生成的域差距。
