Using a hierarchical genetic algorithm, the method evolves adversarial inputs to maximize response length and overthinking markers, targeting LRMs’ tendency to generate redundant reasoning under inconsistent inputs. Experiments across four state-of-the-art models show the attack can increase output length by up to 26.1x on MATH, outperforming both benign and manually crafted baselines, and demonstrates strong transferability from small proxy models to large commercial LRMs.
鉴于大型推理模型（LRMs）在面对逻辑不一致输入时易产生冗长推理，导致资源耗尽，本文提出一种黑盒拒绝服务攻击框架，通过系统性扰动问题结构诱导模型过度思考。该方法采用分层遗传算法优化复合适应度函数，以最大化输出长度与过度思考指标。在四项主流模型上，攻击使输出长度最多提升26.1倍（MATH基准），显著优于良性及人工构造基线，并证明从小型代理模型演化出的对抗输入可有效迁移到大型商业LRMs，揭示过度思考是现代推理系统共有的可利用漏洞。
Novel Dynamic Batch-Sensitive Adam Optimiser for Vehicular Accident Injury Severity Prediction
Authors: Daniel Asare Kyei, Alimatu Saadia-Yussiff, Maame G. Asante-Mensah, Abdul Lateef-Yussiff, Charles Roland Haruna, Derry Emmanuel
First: 2026-05-14T17:06:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:06:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The choice of optimiser is important in deep learning, as it strongly influences model efficiency and speed of convergence. However, many commonly used optimisers encounter difficulties when applied to imbalanced and sequential datasets, limiting their ability to capture patterns of minority classes. In this study, we propose Dynamic Batch-Sensitive Adam (DBS-Adam), an optimiser that dynamically scales the learning rate using a batch difficulty score derived from exponential moving averages of gradient norms and batch loss. DBS-Adam improves training stability and accelerates convergence by increasing updates for difficult batches and reducing them for easier ones. We evaluate DBS-Adam by integrating it with Bi-Directional LSTM networks for accident injury severity prediction, addressing class imbalance through SMOTE-ENN resampling and Focal Loss. Four experimental configurations compare baseline Bi-LSTM models and alternative architectures to assess optimiser impact. Rigorous comparison against state-of-the-art optimisers (AMSGrad, AdamW, AdaBound) across five random seeds demonstrated DBS-Adam's competitive performance with statistically significant precision improvements (p=0.020). Results indicate that DBS-Adam outperforms standard optimisation approaches, achieving 95.22% test accuracy, 96.11% precision, 95.28% recall, 95.39% F1-score, and a test loss of 0.0086. The proposed framework enables effective real-time accident severity classification for targeted emergency response and road safety interventions, demonstrating the value of DBS-Adam for learning from imbalanced sequential data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于车辆事故伤害严重程度预测的新型动态批敏感Adam优化器
优化器的选择在深度学习中至关重要，因为它显著影响模型效率和收敛速度。然而，许多常用优化器在应用于不平衡和序列数据集时遇到困难，限制了其捕捉少数类模式的能力。本研究提出动态批敏感Adam（DBS-Adam）优化器，该优化器通过指数移动平均的梯度范数和批次损失推导出的批次难度分数动态调整学习率。DBS-Adam通过增加困难批次的更新、减少简单批次的更新，提升训练稳定性并加速收敛。我们通过将DBS-Adam与双向LSTM网络集成，用于事故伤害严重程度预测，并通过SMOTE-ENN重采样和Focal Loss解决类别不平衡问题。四种实验配置对比基线双向LSTM模型和替代架构，评估优化器的影响。在五个随机种子下与当前最优优化器（AMSGrad、AdamW、AdaBound）的严格比较表明，DBS-Adam表现具有竞争力，精度提升具有统计显著性（p=0.020）。结果表明，DBS-Adam优于标准优化方法，达到95.22%测试准确率、96.11%精确率、95.28%召回率、95.39%F1分数和0.0086测试损失。所提框架可有效实现事故严重程度的实时分类，支持精准应急响应和道路安全干预，凸显DBS-Adam在学习不平衡序列数据中的价值。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of standard optimizers in handling imbalanced and sequential data, this study introduces Dynamic Batch-Sensitive Adam (DBS-Adam), which dynamically adjusts the learning rate based on a batch difficulty score derived from gradient norms and batch loss. Evaluated with Bi-Directional LSTM networks for vehicular accident injury severity prediction, the method incorporates SMOTE-ENN and Focal Loss to address class imbalance. Experimental comparisons across four configurations and against state-of-the-art optimizers (AMSGrad, AdamW, AdaBound) across five random seeds show DBS-Adam achieves statistically significant precision gains (p=0.020), with test metrics including 95.22% accuracy, 96.11% precision, 95.28% recall, 95.39% F1-score, and 0.0086 test loss, demonstrating superior convergence and stability for imbalanced sequential tasks.
为应对传统优化器在不平衡和序列数据中表现不佳的问题，本文提出动态批处理敏感Adam（DBS-Adam），其根据梯度范数与批次损失的指数移动平均值动态调整学习率。在双向LSTM网络中用于车辆事故伤情严重性预测时，DBS-Adam结合SMOTE-ENN重采样与Focal Loss处理类别不平衡。在四组实验配置中与AMSGrad、AdamW、AdaBound等前沿优化器对比（五次随机种子），DBS-Adam表现显著优于基准方法（p=0.020），测试指标达95.22%准确率、96.11%精确率、95.28%召回率、95.39%F1分数及0.0086损失，证明其在不平衡序列数据中具有更优收敛性与稳定性。
Average Gradient Outer Product in kernel regression provably recovers the central subspace for multi-index models
Authors: Libin Zhu, Damek Davis, Dmitriy Drusvyatskiy, Maryam Fazel
First: 2026-05-14T17:05:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:05:30+00:00
Comments: 95 pages, 12 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study a prototypical situation when a learned predictor can discover useful low-dimensional structure in data, while using fewer samples than are needed for accurate prediction. Specifically, we consider the problem of recovering a multi-index polynomial $f^*(x)=h(Ux)$, with $U\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times d}$ and $r\ll d$, from finitely many data/label pairs. Importantly, the target function depends on input $x$ only through the projection onto an unknown $r$-dimensional central subspace. The algorithm we analyze is appealingly simple: fit kernel ridge regression (KRR) to the data and compute the Average Gradient Outer Product (AGOP) from the fitted predictor. Our main results show that under reasonable assumptions the top $r$-dimensional eigenspace of AGOP provably recovers the central subspace, even in regimes when the prediction error remains large. Specifically, if the target function $f^*$ has degree $p^*$, it is known that $n\asymp d^{p^*}$ samples are necessary for KRR to achieve accurate prediction. In contrast, we show that if a low degree $p$ component of $f^*$ already carries all relevant directions for prediction, subspace recovery occurs in the much lower sample regime $n\asymp d^{p+δ}$ for any $δ\in(0,1)$. Our results thus demonstrate a separation between prediction and representation, and provide an explanation for why iterative kernel methods such as Recursive Feature Machines (RFM) can be sample-efficient in practice.
中文标题/摘要
标题：核回归中平均梯度外积可证明恢复多指标模型的中心子空间
我们研究了一种典型情形：学习的预测器能在样本数少于准确预测所需的情况下，发现数据中的有用低维结构。具体而言，我们考虑从有限的数据-标签对中恢复多指标多项式函数 $f^*(x)=h(Ux)$，其中 $U\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times d}$ 且 $r\ll d$。重要的是，目标函数仅通过输入 $x$ 在未知的 $r$ 维中心子空间上的投影来依赖于输入。我们分析的算法简单直观：对数据拟合核岭回归（KRR），并从拟合预测器中计算平均梯度外积（AGOP）。我们的主要结果表明，在合理假设下，AGOP 的前 $r$ 维特征空间可证明恢复中心子空间，即使在预测误差仍较大的情形下亦然。具体而言，若目标函数 $f^*$ 的次数为 $p^*$，已知 KRR 达到准确预测需 $n\asymp d^{p^*}$ 个样本。相比之下，我们证明：若 $f^*$ 的低次 $p$ 阶分量已包含所有预测相关方向，则子空间恢复可在更低的样本复杂度 $n\asymp d^{p+δ}$ 下实现，其中任意 $δ\in(0,1)$。我们的结果揭示了预测与表示之间的分离，并解释了为何迭代核方法（如递归特征机 RFM）在实践中具有样本效率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to recover low-dimensional structure in high-dimensional data using fewer samples than required for accurate prediction, this paper analyzes a kernel ridge regression approach that leverages the Average Gradient Outer Product (AGOP) to identify the central subspace in multi-index models. The method fits KRR to data and extracts the top r-dimensional eigenspace of AGOP, which provably recovers the unknown r-dimensional subspace even when prediction error is large. Experimental results show that subspace recovery occurs with sample complexity n ≈ d^{p+δ} for any δ ∈ (0,1), where p is the degree of the relevant component of the target function, contrasting with the n ≈ d^{p*} samples needed for accurate prediction when the full degree p* is required. This demonstrates a theoretical separation between prediction and representation, explaining the sample efficiency of iterative kernel methods like RFM.
为在高维数据中用少于预测所需样本数恢复低维结构，本文分析了一种基于核岭回归并计算平均梯度外积（AGOP）的方法，旨在识别多指标模型中的中心子空间——目标函数仅依赖于输入在未知低维子空间上的投影。主要结果表明，在合理假设下，AGOP的前r个特征向量空间可准确恢复该中心子空间，即使预测误差仍较大。关键在于，若目标函数的相关预测方向由低阶分量承载，则子空间恢复所需样本数仅为n ∝ d^{p+δ}（δ ∈ (0,1)），远低于预测所需的n ∝ d^{p*}，揭示了表示与预测之间的分离现象。
ML-Embed: Inclusive and Efficient Embeddings for a Multilingual World
Authors: Ziyin Zhang, Zihan Liao, Hang Yu, Peng Di, Rui Wang
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-14T17:05:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:05:26+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICML 2026. The data has been released earlier in the preprint arXiv:2603.19223
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The development of high-quality text embeddings is increasingly drifting toward an exclusionary future, defined by three critical barriers: prohibitive computational costs, a narrow linguistic focus that neglects most of the world's languages, and a lack of transparency from closed-source or open-weight models that stifles research. To dismantle these barriers, we introduce ML-Embed, a suite of inclusive and efficient models built upon a new framework: 3-Dimensional Matryoshka Learning (3D-ML). Our framework addresses the computational challenge with comprehensive efficiency across the entire model lifecycle. Beyond the storage benefits of Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) and flexible inference-time depth provided by Matryoshka Layer Learning (MLL), we introduce Matryoshka Embedding Learning (MEL) for enhanced parameter efficiency. To address the linguistic challenge, we curate a massively multilingual dataset and train a suite of models ranging from 140M to 8B parameters. In a direct commitment to transparency, we release all models, data, and code. Extensive evaluation on 430 tasks demonstrates that our models set new records on 9 of 17 evaluated MTEB benchmarks, with particularly strong results in low-resource languages, providing a reproducible blueprint for building globally equitable and computationally efficient AI systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ML-Embed：面向多语言世界的包容且高效的嵌入模型
高质量文本嵌入的发展正日益走向排他性未来，受限于三大关键障碍：高昂的计算成本、狭窄的语言聚焦（忽略全球多数语言）以及闭源或开放权重模型缺乏透明度，阻碍了研究进展。为打破这些障碍，我们提出了ML-Embed，一套基于全新框架——三维套娃学习（3D-ML）的包容且高效模型。该框架通过覆盖模型全生命周期的全面效率解决计算挑战。除套娃表示学习（MRL）带来的存储优势和套娃层学习（MLL）提供的灵活推理深度外，我们引入套娃嵌入学习（MEL）以提升参数效率。为应对语言挑战，我们构建了大规模多语言数据集，并训练了从1.4亿到80亿参数不等的模型系列。为践行透明原则，我们公开所有模型、数据与代码。在430项任务上的广泛评估表明，我们的模型在17个MTEB基准中的9个上创下新纪录，尤其在低资源语言中表现强劲，为构建全球公平且计算高效的AI系统提供了可复现的蓝图。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the exclusionary trends in multilingual text embedding development—characterized by high costs, linguistic bias, and lack of transparency—the authors introduce ML-Embed, a suite of efficient and inclusive models built on the 3D-Matryoshka Learning framework. This framework enhances computational efficiency through Matryoshka Representation Learning, Layer Learning, and Embedding Learning, enabling parameter-efficient training and inference. Trained on a massive multilingual dataset and scaled from 140M to 8B parameters, the models are fully open-sourced. Experimental results across 430 tasks show ML-Embed achieves state-of-the-art performance on 9 of 17 MTEB benchmarks, with especially strong results in low-resource languages, demonstrating both technical efficacy and global inclusivity.
为应对当前文本嵌入发展中的排他性问题——包括高昂成本、语言覆盖不足和模型不透明——本文提出ML-Embed，一套基于3D-Matryoshka学习框架的高效包容型多语言模型。该框架通过嵌入层学习、表示学习和嵌入学习三重机制，显著降低计算与存储开销并支持灵活推理。模型在大规模多语言数据集上训练，参数规模从1.4亿至80亿不等，全部开源。在430项任务评估中，ML-Embed在17个MTEB基准中的9个上达到最优性能，尤其在低资源语言上表现突出，验证了其全球包容性与计算效率。
Croissant Baker: Metadata Generation for Discoverable, Governable, and Reusable ML Datasets
Authors: Rafi Al Attrach, Rajna Fani, Sebastian Lobentanzer, Joan Giner-Miguelez, Debanshu Das, Varuni H. K., Nobin Sarwar, Rajat Ghosh, Anwai Archit, Surbhi Motghare, Christina Conrad Parry, Luis Oala, Lara Grosso, Joaquin Vanschoren, Steffen Vogler, Sujata Goswami, Eric S. Rosenthal, Marzyeh Ghassemi, Matthew McDermott, Tom Pollard
First: 2026-05-14T17:04:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:04:39+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 5 figures, 11 tables. Project: https://lcp.mit.edu/croissant-baker/ Code: https://github.com/MIT-LCP/croissant-baker
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Croissant has emerged as the metadata standard for machine learning datasets, providing a structured, JSON-LD-based format that makes dataset discovery, automated ingestion, and reproducible analysis machine-checkable across ML platforms. Adoption has accelerated, and NeurIPS now requires Croissant metadata in every submission to its dataset tracks. Yet in practice Croissant generation usually starts with uploading data to a public platform, a path infeasible for governed and large local repositories that hold much of the high-value data ML increasingly relies on. We release Croissant Baker, a local-first, open-source command-line tool that generates validated Croissant metadata directly from a dataset directory through a modular handler registry. We evaluate Croissant Baker on over 140 datasets, scaling to MIMIC-IV at 886 million rows and 374 Parquet files. On held-out comparisons against producer-authored or standards-derived ground truth, Croissant Baker reaches 97-100% agreement across multiple domains.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Croissant Baker：用于可发现、可管理且可重用的机器学习数据集的元数据生成
Croissant 已成为机器学习数据集的元数据标准，提供基于 JSON-LD 的结构化格式，使数据集发现、自动化摄取和可重现分析在不同机器学习平台间实现机器可验证。其采用加速，NeurIPS 现在要求所有数据集投稿必须包含 Croissant 元数据。然而，实践中 Croissant 生成通常始于将数据上传至公共平台，这对受管理且规模庞大的本地仓库（包含机器学习日益依赖的高价值数据）而言不可行。我们发布 Croissant Baker，一款本地优先、开源的命令行工具，可通过模块化处理器注册表，直接从数据集目录生成经过验证的 Croissant 元数据。我们在 140 多个数据集上评估 Croissant Baker，支持规模达 8.86 亿行和 374 个 Parquet 文件的 MIMIC-IV 数据集。在与生产者编写或标准推导的基准对比中，Croissant Baker 在多个领域达成 97-100% 的一致性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for standardized, machine-readable metadata to support discoverability and reproducibility of ML datasets, especially for governed local repositories that cannot rely on public platform uploads, the authors introduce Croissant Baker: an open-source command-line tool that generates validated Croissant metadata directly from local dataset directories using a modular handler registry. Evaluated across 140 datasets including the massive MIMIC-IV corpus, Croissant Baker demonstrates high fidelity, achieving 97-100% agreement with ground truth metadata from dataset producers or standards-derived sources across multiple domains, validating its utility for scalable, local metadata generation.
为支持机器学习数据集的可发现性和可重复性，尤其针对无法依赖公共平台上传的受控本地数据仓库，作者提出了Croissant Baker：一个开源命令行工具，通过模块化处理器注册表，直接从本地数据目录生成经过验证的Croissant元数据。在140个数据集（包括规模达8.86亿行、374个Parquet文件的MIMIC-IV）上评估，该工具在多个领域与数据生产者或标准推导的元数据达成97-100%的一致性，验证了其在实际应用中的可靠性。
Prompting from the bench: Large-scale pretraining is not sufficient to prepare LLMs for ordinary meaning analysis
Authors: Abhishek Purushothama, Junghyun Min, Brandon Waldon, Nathan Schneider
First: 2025-10-29T10:21:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:03:29+00:00
Comments: Accepted FAccT 2026; 29 pages, 14 tables, 7 figures. Previous title - Not ready for the bench: LLM legal interpretation is unstable and out of step with human judgments; NLLPW 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In the U.S. judicial system, a widespread approach to legal interpretation entails assessing how a legal text would be understood by an `ordinary' speaker of the language. Recent scholarship has proposed that legal practitioners leverage large language models (LLMs) to ascertain a text's ordinary meaning. But are LLMs up to the task? As textual interpretation questions arise in spheres ranging from criminal law to civil rights, we argue it is crucial that models not be taken as authoritative without rigorous evaluation. This work offers an empirical argument against LLM-assisted interpretation as recently practiced by legal scholars and federal judges, who reasoned the large amount of data that models see in training would enable models to illuminate how people ordinarily use certain words or phrases. In controlled experiments, we find failures in robustness which cast doubt on this assumption and raise serious questions about the utility of these models in practice. For the models in our evaluation, slight changes to the format of a question can lead to wildly different conclusions -- a vulnerability that parties with an interest in the outcome could exploit. Comparing with a dataset where people were asked similar legal interpretation questions, we see that these models are at best moderately correlated to human judgments -- not strong enough given the stakes in this domain.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从席位出发的提示：大规模预训练不足以使大语言模型胜任普通含义分析
在美国司法体系中，一种广泛采用的法律解释方法是评估法律文本如何被该语言的‘普通’使用者理解。近期学术研究提出，法律从业者可借助大语言模型（LLMs）来确定文本的普通含义。但LLMs真的胜任此任务吗？随着刑事法律到民权领域等各领域出现文本解释问题，我们认为在未经严格评估前，不应将模型视为权威。本研究通过实证论证反对法律学者和联邦法官近期所采用的LLM辅助解释方法，他们认为模型在训练中所见大量数据能揭示人们如何通常使用某些词语或短语。在受控实验中，我们发现模型稳健性不足，这质疑了该假设，并对这些模型在实践中的效用提出严重质疑。在我们的评估中，模型对问题格式的微小改动可能产生截然不同的结论——这为利益相关方可能利用的漏洞。与一组让人类回答类似法律解释问题的数据对比，我们发现这些模型最多仅与人类判断中度相关——鉴于该领域的高风险，相关性不足。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing use of large language models (LLMs) in legal interpretation tasks, this study challenges the assumption that pretraining on vast corpora equips LLMs to reliably capture ordinary meaning as required in U.S. jurisprudence. Through controlled experiments, the authors demonstrate that LLMs exhibit significant instability: minor variations in question formatting can lead to drastically different outputs, exposing a vulnerability exploitable in high-stakes legal contexts. Furthermore, correlation with human judgments on a parallel dataset was found to be only moderate at best, suggesting LLMs are not yet sufficiently aligned with human interpretation norms to serve as authoritative tools in legal reasoning.
鉴于大语言模型（LLM）在法律解释中的日益应用，本研究质疑仅靠大规模预训练即可使模型准确捕捉法律语境下‘普通语义’的假设。通过控制实验发现，LLM对问题措辞的微小变动极为敏感，可能导致输出结果剧烈波动，暴露其在高风险法律场景中易被操控的脆弱性。与人类判断数据集对比显示，模型输出与人类判断仅呈中等相关性，不足以支撑其在法律实践中的权威性。研究结论表明，当前LLM尚未具备可靠承担法律语义分析任务的能力。
Concurrency without Model Changes: Future-based Asynchronous Function Calling for LLMs
Authors: Guangyu Feng, Huanzhi Mao, Prabal Dutta, Joseph E. Gonzalez
First: 2026-05-14T17:02:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:02:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Function calling, also known as tool use, is a core capability of modern LLM agents but is typically constrained by synchronous execution semantics. Under these semantics, LLM decoding is blocked until each function call completes, resulting in increasing end-to-end latency. In this work, we introduce AsyncFC, a pure execution-layer framework that decouples LLM decoding from function execution, enabling overlap between model decoding and function execution as well as inter-function parallelism when dependencies permit. AsyncFC layers over existing models and unmodified function implementations, requiring no fine-tuning or changes to the standard synchronous function-calling protocol. Across standard function-calling benchmarks and adapted software engineering benchmarks, AsyncFC significantly reduces end-to-end task completion time while preserving task accuracy. Furthermore, these results reveal that LLMs possess a native capability to reason over symbolic futures that represent unresolved execution results, enabling an asynchronous paradigm for model-tool interaction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无需模型变更的并发性：面向LLM的基于未来的异步函数调用
函数调用，也称为工具使用，是现代LLM代理的核心能力，但通常受限于同步执行语义。在这些语义下，LLM解码会阻塞，直到每个函数调用完成，导致端到端延迟增加。在本研究中，我们引入AsyncFC，一个纯执行层框架，将LLM解码与函数执行解耦，允许在依赖允许的情况下，模型解码与函数执行重叠以及函数间并行。AsyncFC可叠加在现有模型和未修改的函数实现之上，无需微调或更改标准同步函数调用协议。在标准函数调用基准和改编的软件工程基准上，AsyncFC显著减少了端到端任务完成时间，同时保持任务准确性。此外，这些结果表明LLM具备原生推理能力，能够处理代表未解决执行结果的符号未来，从而实现模型-工具交互的异步范式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the latency bottlenecks caused by synchronous function calling in LLM agents, this work introduces AsyncFC, a framework that decouples LLM decoding from function execution to enable parallelism and overlap. By layering over existing models and unmodified functions without requiring fine-tuning or protocol changes, AsyncFC allows the model to reason about symbolic futures representing pending function results, thereby supporting asynchronous execution. Experimental results across standard and adapted benchmarks show significant reductions in end-to-end task completion time while maintaining task accuracy, demonstrating that LLMs inherently support reasoning over futures for asynchronous tool interaction.
为了解决LLM代理中同步函数调用导致的延迟问题，本研究提出了AsyncFC框架，该框架将模型解码与函数执行解耦，实现并行与重叠执行。AsyncFC无需微调或修改现有函数接口，仅通过在现有模型上叠加实现，即可支持模型对未完成函数结果的符号化未来推理。实验表明，在多个基准测试中，AsyncFC显著缩短了端到端任务完成时间，同时保持任务准确率，揭示了LLM天然具备对未决执行结果进行异步推理的能力。
From User Preferences to Base Score Extraction Functions in Gradual Argumentation (with Appendix)
Authors: Aniol Civit, Antonio Rago, Antonio Andriella, Guillem Alenyà, Francesca Toni
First: 2026-02-16T12:01:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:01:45+00:00
Comments: Accepted to AAMAS 2026 - With Appendix
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Gradual argumentation is a field of symbolic AI which is attracting attention for its ability to support transparent and contestable AI systems. It is considered a useful tool in domains such as decision-making, recommendation, debate analysis, and others. The outcomes in such domains are usually dependent on the arguments' base scores, which must be selected carefully. Often, this selection process requires user expertise and may not always be straightforward. On the other hand, organising the arguments by preference could simplify the task. In this work, we introduce \emph{Base Score Extraction Functions}, which provide a mapping from users' preferences over arguments to base scores. These functions can be applied to the arguments of a \emph{Bipolar Argumentation Framework} (BAF), supplemented with preferences, to obtain a \emph{Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Framework} (QBAF), allowing the use of well-established computational tools in gradual argumentation. We outline the desirable properties of base score extraction functions, discuss some design choices, and provide an algorithm for base score extraction. Our method incorporates an approximation of non-linearities in human preferences to allow for better approximation of the real ones. Finally, we evaluate our approach both theoretically and experimentally in a robotics setting, and offer recommendations for selecting appropriate gradual semantics in practice.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从用户偏好到渐进论证中的基础得分提取函数（附录）
渐进论证是符号人工智能的一个领域，因其支持透明且可争议的AI系统而受到关注。它在决策、推荐、辩论分析等领域被视为有用工具。这些领域的结果通常依赖于论证的基础得分，需谨慎选择。通常，此选择过程需要用户专业知识，且未必直观。另一方面，按偏好组织论证可简化任务。本文引入了\emph{基础得分提取函数}，将用户对论证的偏好映射为基础得分。这些函数可应用于\emph{双极论证框架}（BAF），并结合偏好，生成\emph{量化双极论证框架}（QBAF），从而可使用渐进论证中成熟的计算工具。我们概述了基础得分提取函数的期望属性，讨论了部分设计选择，并提供了一种基础得分提取算法。该方法通过近似人类偏好的非线性特性，以更好地逼近真实偏好。最后，我们在机器人领域从理论和实验两方面评估了该方法，并提供了实践中选择合适渐进语义的建议。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate the selection of base scores in gradual argumentation systems without requiring expert user input, this work introduces Base Score Extraction Functions that map user preferences over arguments to quantitative scores. These functions enable the transformation of a Bipolar Argumentation Framework into a Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Framework, allowing the use of established computational tools. The method approximates non-linear human preferences to improve accuracy and includes an algorithm for score extraction. Experimental evaluation in a robotics setting demonstrates the effectiveness of the approach in aligning argument outcomes with user preferences, while theoretical analysis supports its soundness and provides practical guidance for selecting appropriate gradual semantics.
为解决渐进论证系统中人工选择基础分数的复杂性，本文提出基础分数提取函数，将用户对论点的偏好映射为量化基础分数。该方法可将双极论证框架转化为量化双极论证框架，从而支持现有计算工具的应用。方法通过近似人类偏好的非线性特征提升准确性，并提供提取算法。在机器人场景中的实验验证了该方法能有效匹配用户偏好，理论分析也支持其合理性，并为实际选择渐进语义提供了指导建议。
BiTrajDiff: Bidirectional Trajectory Generation with Diffusion Models for Offline Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Yunpeng Qing, Yixiao Chi, Shuo Chen, Shunyu Liu, Kexuan Zhou, Sixu Lin, Litao Liu, Changqing Zou
First: 2025-06-06T05:41:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:01:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in offline Reinforcement Learning (RL) have proven that effective policy learning can benefit from imposing conservative constraints on pre-collected datasets. However, such static datasets often exhibit distribution bias, resulting in limited generalizability. To address this limitation, a straightforward solution is data augmentation (DA), which leverages generative models to enrich data distribution. Despite the promising results, current DA techniques focus solely on reconstructing future trajectories from given states, while ignoring the exploration of history transitions that reach them. This single-direction paradigm inevitably hinders the discovery of diverse behavior patterns, especially those leading to critical states that may have yielded high-reward outcomes. In this work, we introduce Bidirectional Trajectory Diffusion (BiTrajDiff), a novel DA framework for offline RL that models both future and history trajectories from any intermediate states. Specifically, we decompose the trajectory generation task into two independent yet complementary diffusion processes: one generating forward trajectories to predict future dynamics, and the other generating backward trajectories to trace essential history transitions.BiTrajDiff can efficiently leverage critical states as anchors to expand into potentially valuable yet underexplored regions of the state space, thereby facilitating dataset diversity. Extensive experiments on the D4RL benchmark suite demonstrate that BiTrajDiff achieves superior performance compared to other advanced DA methods across various offline RL backbones.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BiTrajDiff：基于扩散模型的双向轨迹生成用于离线强化学习
近期离线强化学习（RL）的进展表明，通过对预收集数据集施加保守约束可有效提升策略学习效果。然而，此类静态数据集常存在分布偏倚，导致泛化能力受限。为解决此问题，一种直接方案是数据增强（DA），即利用生成模型丰富数据分布。尽管效果显著，现有DA技术仅关注从给定状态重建未来轨迹，而忽略到达这些状态的历史转移探索。这种单向范式必然限制多样行为模式的发现，尤其难以捕捉通往关键状态（可能带来高奖励）的路径。本文提出双向轨迹扩散（BiTrajDiff），一种面向离线RL的新型DA框架，可从任意中间状态建模未来与历史轨迹。具体而言，我们将轨迹生成任务分解为两个独立互补的扩散过程：一个生成前向轨迹以预测未来动态，另一个生成后向轨迹以追溯关键历史转移。BiTrajDiff可高效利用关键状态作为锚点，扩展至潜在有价值但未充分探索的状态空间区域，从而促进数据集多样性。在D4RL基准套件上的大量实验表明，BiTrajDiff在多种离线RL骨干模型上均优于其他先进DA方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the distribution bias in static offline RL datasets that limits policy generalization, this work proposes BiTrajDiff, a bidirectional trajectory generation framework using diffusion models to augment data. It addresses the limitation of existing methods that only model forward trajectories by introducing two complementary diffusion processes: one generating future trajectories from states and another reconstructing past trajectories to trace critical transitions. Experiments on the D4RL benchmark show BiTrajDiff consistently outperforms state-of-the-art data augmentation methods across multiple offline RL algorithms, demonstrating its effectiveness in expanding dataset diversity and improving policy performance.
为解决离线强化学习中数据分布偏置导致的泛化能力不足问题，本文提出BiTrajDiff框架，利用扩散模型双向生成轨迹以增强数据多样性。该方法突破现有仅建模前向轨迹的局限，设计两个互补的扩散过程：一个生成从当前状态出发的未来轨迹，另一个重建到达该状态的历史轨迹。通过以关键状态为锚点，模型可探索未充分覆盖的状态空间区域，提升数据多样性。在D4RL基准上的实验表明，BiTrajDiff在多种离线强化学习算法上均优于现有最先进的数据增强方法。
SOCC-ICP: Semantics-Assisted Odometry based on Occupancy Grids and ICP
Authors: Johannes Scherer, Sebastian Hirt, Henri Meeß
First: 2026-05-14T17:00:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:00:38+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 3 figures, Accepted May 2026 for publication in IEEE Robotics and Automation Letters (RA-L)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reliable pose estimation in previously unseen environments is a fundamental capability of autonomous systems. Existing LiDAR odometry methods typically employ point-, surfel-, or NDT-based map representations, which are distinct from the semantic occupancy grids commonly used for downstream tasks such as motion planning. We introduce SOCC-ICP, a semantics-assisted odometry framework that jointly performs Semantic OCCupancy grid mapping and LiDAR scan alignment. Each map voxel encodes geometric and semantic statistics, enabling adaptive point-to-point or point-to-plane ICP based on local planarity. Further, the occupancy grid naturally filters dynamic objects through raycasting-based free-space updates. Across diverse evaluation scenarios, SOCC-ICP achieves performance competitive with state-of-the-art LiDAR odometry and remains robust in geometrically degenerate environments, even in the absence of semantic cues. When semantic labels are available, integrating them into map construction, downsampling, and correspondence weighting yields further accuracy gains. By unifying odometry and semantic occupancy grid mapping within a single representation, SOCC-ICP eliminates redundant map structures and directly provides a map suitable for downstream robotic applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SOCC-ICP：基于占据网格和ICP的语义辅助里程计
在之前未见过的环境中实现可靠的位姿估计是自主系统的基本能力。现有激光雷达里程计方法通常采用点、表面元或NDT地图表示，与用于运动规划等下游任务的语义占据网格不同。我们提出了SOCC-ICP，一种语义辅助里程计框架，联合执行语义占据网格建图和激光雷达扫描对齐。每个地图体素编码几何和语义统计信息，支持根据局部平面性自适应选择点到点或点到平面ICP。此外，占据网格通过基于射线投射的自由空间更新自然过滤动态物体。在多种评估场景中，SOCC-ICP性能与当前最先进的激光雷达里程计相当，即使在缺乏语义线索的情况下，也能在几何退化环境中保持鲁棒性。当语义标签可用时，将其整合到地图构建、下采样和对应权重计算中可进一步提升精度。通过将里程计与语义占据网格建图统一于单一表示中，SOCC-ICP消除了冗余地图结构，并直接提供适用于下游机器人应用的地图。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable pose estimation in novel environments and the gap between LiDAR odometry methods and semantic occupancy grids used in downstream tasks, SOCC-ICP introduces a unified framework that jointly builds semantic occupancy grids and performs LiDAR scan alignment. Each voxel stores geometric and semantic statistics to enable adaptive ICP—point-to-point or point-to-plane—based on local surface properties, while raycasting filters out dynamic objects. Experimental results show SOCC-ICP matches or exceeds state-of-the-art LiDAR odometry in diverse scenarios, including geometrically degenerate environments without semantic labels, and achieves further accuracy gains when semantic labels are available, eliminating redundant map structures and directly outputting a map usable for robotics applications.
为解决在未知环境中实现可靠位姿估计的需求，并弥合现有LiDAR里程计方法与下游任务常用语义占据网格之间的差距，SOCC-ICP提出了一种联合构建语义占据网格与LiDAR扫描对齐的统一框架。每个网格体素存储几何与语义统计信息，支持根据局部平面性自适应选择点到点或点到平面ICP，同时通过射线投射过滤动态物体。实验表明，SOCC-ICP在多种场景下性能可媲美或超越现有最先进LiDAR里程计方法，即使在无语义信息的几何退化环境中仍保持鲁棒性；当提供语义标签时，其在地图构建、下采样和对应权重计算中进一步提升精度，消除冗余地图结构，直接输出适用于机器人下游任务的地图。
Time Series Forecasting Through the Lens of Dynamics
Authors: Alexis-Raja Brachet, Pierre-Yves Richard, Céline Hudelot
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-07-21T16:29:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:58:20+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While deep learning is facing an homogenization across modalities led by Transformers, they are still challenged by shallow linear models in the time series forecasting task. Our hypothesis is that models should learn a direct link from past to future data points, which we identify as a learning dynamics capability. We develop an original $\texttt{PRO-DYN}$ nomenclature to analyze existing models through the lens of dynamics. Two observations thus emerge: $\textbf{1.}$ under-performing architectures learn dynamics at most partially, $\textbf{2.}$ the location of the dynamics block at the model end is of prime importance. Our systemic and empirical studies both confirm our observations on a set of performance-varying models with diverse backbones. We propose a simple plug-and-play methodology guiding model designs and improvements.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过动力学视角的时间序列预测
尽管深度学习在多模态领域正面临由Transformer主导的同质化趋势，但在时间序列预测任务中，它们仍被浅层线性模型所挑战。我们的假设是，模型应直接学习从过去数据点到未来数据点的映射，这我们称之为学习动力学的能力。我们提出了一种原创的$\texttt{PRO-DYN}$命名法，通过动力学视角分析现有模型。由此得出两个观察：$\textbf{1.}$ 表现不佳的架构最多只能部分学习动力学；$\textbf{2.}$ 动力学模块在模型末端的位置至关重要。我们的系统性与实证研究均在一组性能各异、骨干结构多样的模型上验证了上述观察。我们提出了一种简单易用的插件式方法，指导模型设计与改进。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that deep learning models, despite their success, still underperform compared to shallow linear models in time series forecasting, the authors hypothesize that effective models must explicitly learn dynamics — a direct mapping from past to future data points. They introduce a novel analytical framework called PRO-DYN to evaluate existing architectures through this lens, revealing two key findings: first, underperforming models capture dynamics only partially; second, the placement of the dynamics module near the end of the model significantly impacts performance. Empirical validation across diverse model backbones confirms these insights, leading to a simple plug-and-play methodology for improving model design in time series forecasting.
鉴于深度学习模型在多数领域占据主导地位，但在时间序列预测任务中仍被浅层线性模型超越，作者假设模型应能显式学习从过去到未来的动态映射。他们提出PRO-DYN框架，从动态视角分析现有架构，发现两点关键结论：一是表现不佳的模型仅部分捕捉动态；二是动态模块置于模型末端对性能至关重要。通过在多种骨干网络上的系统性实证研究，验证了上述发现，并提出一种简单易用的插件式方法，指导时间序列预测模型的设计与改进。
Projected gradient methods for nonconvex and stochastic smooth optimization: new complexities and auto-conditioned stepsizes
Authors: Guanghui Lan, Tianjiao Li, Yangyang Xu
First: 2024-12-18T19:34:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:58:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a novel class of projected gradient (PG) methods for minimizing a smooth but not necessarily convex function over a convex compact set. We first provide a novel analysis of the constant-stepsize PG method, achieving the best-known iteration complexity for finding an approximate stationary point of the problem. We then develop an "auto-conditioned" projected gradient (AC-PG) variant that achieves the same iteration complexity without requiring the input of the Lipschitz constant of the gradient or any line search procedure. The key idea is to estimate the Lipschitz constant using first-order information gathered from the previous iterations, and to show that the error caused by underestimating the Lipschitz constant can be properly controlled. We then generalize the PG methods to the stochastic setting, by proposing a stochastic projected gradient (SPG) method and a variance-reduced stochastic gradient (VR-SPG) method, achieving new complexity bounds in different oracle settings. We also present auto-conditioned stepsize policies for both stochastic PG methods and establish comparable convergence guarantees.
中文标题/摘要
标题：非凸与随机光滑优化的投影梯度方法：新复杂度与自适应步长
我们提出了一类用于在凸紧集上最小化光滑但未必凸函数的新型投影梯度（PG）方法。首先，我们对常数步长PG方法进行了新颖分析，实现了寻找问题近似驻点的最佳已知迭代复杂度。接着，我们开发了一种“自适应条件”投影梯度（AC-PG）变体，在无需输入梯度Lipschitz常数或任何线搜索过程的情况下，达到相同迭代复杂度。其核心思想是利用前几次迭代收集的一阶信息来估计Lipschitz常数，并证明低估该常数所引起的误差可被有效控制。随后，我们将PG方法推广到随机设置，提出随机投影梯度（SPG）方法和方差缩减随机梯度（VR-SPG）方法，在不同算子设置下获得新的复杂度界。同时，我们为两种随机PG方法提供了自适应步长策略，并建立了可比的收敛保证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient nonconvex optimization without prior knowledge of gradient Lipschitz constants, this paper introduces projected gradient methods with auto-conditioned stepsizes. It first analyzes a constant-stepsize projected gradient method, achieving the best-known iteration complexity for finding approximate stationary points. Then, it proposes an auto-conditioned projected gradient (AC-PG) method that eliminates the need for explicit Lipschitz constant input or line search by adaptively estimating it from past gradients and controlling estimation errors. The methods are further extended to stochastic settings via stochastic projected gradient (SPG) and variance-reduced (VR-SPG) variants, yielding new complexity bounds under different oracle models, with auto-conditioned stepsize policies preserving comparable convergence guarantees.
为解决非凸优化中无需预先知道梯度Lipschitz常数的问题，本文提出了一类自适应步长的投影梯度方法。首先，对常数步长投影梯度方法进行新分析，获得寻找近似驻点的最佳迭代复杂度。随后，提出自适应条件投影梯度（AC-PG）变体，通过从历史梯度中估计Lipschitz常数并控制估计误差，无需输入梯度Lipschitz常数或线搜索。方法进一步扩展至随机设置，提出随机投影梯度（SPG）和方差缩减随机梯度（VR-SPG）方法，在不同算子模型下获得新的复杂度界，且自适应步长策略保持相近的收敛保证。
On the Cultural Anachronism and Temporal Reasoning in Vision Language Models
Authors: Mukul Ranjan, Prince Jha, Khushboo Kumari, Zhiqiang Shen
First: 2026-05-14T16:58:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:58:16+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://khushboo0012.github.io/tab-vlm-webpage/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly applied to cultural heritage materials, from digital archives to educational platforms. This work identifies a fundamental issue in how these models interpret historical artifacts. We define this phenomenon as cultural anachronism, the tendency to misinterpret historical objects using temporally inappropriate concepts, materials, or cultural frameworks. To quantify this phenomenon, we introduce the Temporal Anachronism Benchmark for Vision-Language Models (TAB-VLM), a dataset of 600 questions across six categories, designed to evaluate temporal reasoning on 1,600 Indian cultural artifacts spanning prehistoric to modern periods. Systematic evaluations of ten state-of-the-art models reveal significant deficiencies on our benchmark, and even the best model (GPT-5.2) achieves only 58.7% overall accuracy. The performance gap persists across varying architectures and scales, suggesting that cultural anachronism represents a significant limitation in visual AI systems, regardless of model size. These findings highlight the disparity between current VLM capabilities and the requirements for accurately interpreting cultural heritage materials, particularly for non-Western visual cultures underrepresented in training data. Our benchmark provides a foundation for enhancing temporal cognition in multimodal AI systems that interact with historical artifacts. The dataset and code are available in our project page.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视觉语言模型中的文化错时与时间推理问题
视觉语言模型（VLMs）日益应用于文化遗产材料，从数字档案到教育平台。本研究识别出这些模型在解读历史文物时存在根本性问题，将其定义为“文化错时”——即倾向于使用时间上不恰当的概念、材料或文化框架来误读历史物件。为量化此现象，我们提出了视觉语言模型时间错时基准（TAB-VLM），包含600个问题，覆盖六个类别，旨在评估1600件印度文化文物（从史前至现代）的时间推理能力。对十种前沿模型的系统评估显示，其在本基准上表现显著不足，最优模型（GPT-5.2）整体准确率仅为58.7%。性能差距在不同架构和规模模型中均持续存在，表明文化错时是视觉AI系统的重要局限，与模型大小无关。这些发现凸显当前VLM能力与准确解读文化遗产材料（特别是训练数据中代表性不足的非西方视觉文化）的需求之间的差距。本基准为提升多模态AI系统对历史文物的时间认知能力提供了基础。数据集与代码可在项目页面获取。

VER: Vision Expert Transformer for Robot Learning via Foundation Distillation and Dynamic Routing
Authors: Yixiao Wang, Mingxiao Huo, Zhixuan Liang, Yushi Du, Lingfeng Sun, Haotian Lin, Jinghuan Shang, Chensheng Peng, Mohit Bansal, Mingyu Ding, Masayoshi Tomizuka
First: 2025-10-06T18:00:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:57:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Pretrained vision foundation models (VFMs) advance robotic learning via rich visual representations, yet individual VFMs typically excel only in specific domains, limiting generality across tasks. Distilling multiple VFMs into a unified representation for policy can mitigate this limitation but often yields inflexible task-specific feature selection and requires costly full re-training to incorporate robot-domain knowledge. We propose VER, a Vision Expert transformer for Robot learning. During pretraining, VER distills multiple VFMs into a vision expert library. It then fine-tunes only a lightweight routing network (fewer than 0.4% of parameters) to dynamically select task-relevant experts from the pretrained library for downstream robot tasks. We further introduce Patchwise Expert Routing with Curriculum Top-K Annealing to improve both flexibility and precision of dynamic expert selection. Moreover, VER supports parameter-efficient finetuning for scalable expert utilization and adaptive robot-domain knowledge integration. Across 17 diverse robotic tasks and multiple policy heads, VER achieves state-of-the-art performance. We find that VER reduces large-norm outliers in task-irrelevant regions (e.g., background) and concentrates on task-critical regions. Visualizations and codes can be found in https://yixiaowang7.github.io/ver_page/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VER：通过基础蒸馏与动态路由的视觉专家变换器用于机器人学习
预训练视觉基础模型（VFMs）通过丰富的视觉表征推动机器人学习，但单个VFM通常仅在特定领域表现优异，限制了跨任务的泛化能力。将多个VFM蒸馏为统一表示以用于策略可缓解此局限，但常导致任务特定特征选择僵化，且需耗费高昂成本的完整再训练以融入机器人领域知识。我们提出VER，一种用于机器人学习的视觉专家变换器。在预训练阶段，VER将多个VFM蒸馏为视觉专家库；随后仅微调轻量级路由网络（参数少于0.4%），动态从预训练库中选择与任务相关的专家用于下游机器人任务。我们进一步引入分块专家路由与课程式Top-K退火机制，以提升动态专家选择的灵活性与精度。此外，VER支持参数高效微调，实现专家的可扩展利用与机器人领域知识的自适应整合。在17个多样化机器人任务及多个策略头中，VER达到最先进性能。我们发现，VER减少了任务无关区域（如背景）中的大范数异常值，并聚焦于任务关键区域。可视化与代码见https://yixiaowang7.github.io/ver_page/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the domain-specific limitations of pretrained vision foundation models, VER proposes a Vision Expert Transformer that distills multiple VFMs into a shared expert library during pretraining, then fine-tunes a lightweight routing network to dynamically select task-relevant experts for robot learning. This approach enables parameter-efficient adaptation and adaptive integration of robot-domain knowledge without full retraining. Experimental results across 17 robotic tasks show VER achieves state-of-the-art performance while reducing large-norm outliers in irrelevant regions and focusing attention on task-critical areas.
为解决预训练视觉基础模型在特定领域表现优异但泛化能力不足的问题，VER提出一种视觉专家Transformer架构，通过预训练阶段将多个视觉基础模型蒸馏为共享的视觉专家库。随后仅微调一个轻量级路由网络（参数量不足0.4%），动态选择与任务相关的专家用于下游机器人任务。VER进一步引入分块专家路由与课程式Top-K退火机制，提升路由灵活性与精度，支持参数高效微调和机器人领域知识的自适应融合。在17个多样化机器人任务中，VER达到最先进性能，同时减少无关区域（如背景）的大范数异常值，聚焦于任务关键区域。
After the Interface: Relocating Human Agency in the Age of Conversational AI
Authors: Mengke Wu, Mike Yao
Venue: ACM Conversational User Interfaces 2026 (CUI '26)
First: 2026-05-14T16:54:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:54:57+00:00
Comments: 7 pages, 1 figure
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As AI systems take on greater autonomy, a quiet anxiety has settled over the HCI community: human agency is eroding. Users no longer control execution, interfaces recede, and machines decide. We argue that this anxiety, while understandable, reflects a framing problem rather than an empirical finding. Agency has not diminished but has relocated. As interaction has shifted from command- and feature-based paradigms toward conversational, generative, and agentic AI, human agency migrates from interface affordances to interaction itself: articulating goals, evaluating outputs, and negotiating outcomes. To make this relocation visible, we revisit control as a diagnostic lens, distinguish process control and outcome control, and map different systems across this space to show that what looks like agency's disappearance is actually its redistribution. We take seriously the objection that outcome-based agency may be illusory in systems that produce plausible but unverifiable outputs, and argue that this concern reveals what agency in human-AI interaction truly requires. This paper invites the CUI community to reconsider what agency means, where it lives, and what it demands, including who gets to have it and who holds responsibility when it fails, before the consequences become impossible to overlook.
中文标题/摘要
标题：界面之后：在对话式人工智能时代重新定位人类能动性
随着AI系统自主性增强，人机交互领域悄然弥漫着一种焦虑：人类能动性正在消退。用户不再掌控执行过程，界面退居幕后，机器做出决策。我们认为，这种焦虑虽可理解，但反映的是框架问题而非实证发现。能动性并未消失，而是发生了转移。随着交互模式从命令与功能导向转向对话式、生成式和代理式AI，人类能动性从界面的可用性迁移到交互本身：明确目标、评估输出、协商结果。为使这种转移可见，我们重新审视控制作为诊断视角，区分过程控制与结果控制，并将不同系统映射到该空间中，以表明看似能动性消失实为能动性的再分配。我们严肃对待反对意见——在生成看似合理但无法验证输出的系统中，基于结果的能动性可能是虚幻的，并认为这一担忧揭示了人类与AI交互中能动性真正所需之本质。本文呼吁对话式用户界面社区重新思考能动性的含义、所在位置、所需条件，包括谁有权拥有它，以及当其失效时谁应承担责任，以免其后果无法忽视。
Summary / 总结
As conversational AI systems grow more autonomous, HCI researchers worry human agency is disappearing, but this concern stems from a framing error rather than empirical loss. The paper argues agency has shifted from interface controls to the user’s role in articulating goals, evaluating outputs, and negotiating outcomes. By redefining control as a diagnostic lens—distinguishing process and outcome control—and mapping systems across this spectrum, the authors show agency is redistributed, not erased. They acknowledge the risk of illusory agency in systems producing unverifiable outputs, suggesting this reveals what true agency demands: accountability and responsibility in human-AI collaboration.
随着对话式AI系统日益自主，人机交互领域担忧人类的自主权正在消退，但该担忧源于认知框架的偏差而非实际丧失。论文主张自主权并未消失，而是从界面控制转向用户在目标表达、结果评估与协商中的角色。通过将控制重新定义为诊断工具，并区分过程控制与结果控制，作者将不同AI系统映射到该框架中，揭示了自主权的再分配而非消亡。他们承认在生成不可验证输出的系统中，结果导向的自主权可能具虚幻性，进而指出理解自主权真实需求——特别是责任归属——至关重要，以免后果不可挽回。
TRIM: Token-wise Attention-Derived Saliency for Data-Efficient Instruction Tuning
Authors: Manish Nagaraj, Sakshi Choudhary, Utkarsh Saxena, Deepak Ravikumar, Kaushik Roy
First: 2025-10-08T15:11:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:54:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Instruction tuning is essential for aligning large language models (LLMs) to downstream tasks and commonly relies on large, diverse corpora. However, small, high-quality subsets, known as coresets, can deliver comparable or superior results, though curating them remains challenging. Existing methods often rely on coarse, sample-level signals like gradients, an approach that is computationally expensive and overlooks fine-grained features. To address this, we introduce TRIM (Token Relevance via Interpretable Multi-layer Attention), a forward-only, token-centric framework. Instead of using gradients, TRIM operates by matching underlying representational patterns identified via attention-based "fingerprints" from a handful of target samples. Such an approach makes TRIM highly efficient and uniquely sensitive to the structural features that define a task. Coresets selected by our method consistently outperform state-of-the-art baselines by up to 9% on downstream tasks and even surpass the performance of full-data fine-tuning in some settings. By avoiding expensive backward passes, TRIM achieves this at a fraction of the computational cost. These findings establish TRIM as a scalable and efficient alternative for building high-quality instruction-tuning datasets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TRIM：基于令牌注意力导出的显著性，用于数据高效的指令调优
指令调优对于将大语言模型（LLM）对齐到下游任务至关重要，通常依赖大规模、多样化的语料库。然而，小规模高质量子集（称为核心集）可提供相当甚至更优的结果，但其筛选仍具挑战性。现有方法常依赖粗粒度、样本级信号（如梯度），计算成本高且忽略细粒度特征。为此，我们提出TRIM（通过可解释多层注意力实现的令牌相关性），一种仅前向传播、以令牌为中心的框架。TRIM不依赖梯度，而是通过注意力生成的“指纹”匹配少量目标样本的底层表征模式。该方法使TRIM高效且对定义任务的结构特征高度敏感。由我们方法选出的核心集在下游任务中持续优于现有最先进基线高达9%，并在某些情况下超越全数据微调性能。通过避免昂贵的反向传播，TRIM以极低计算成本实现此目标。这些发现确立TRIM为构建高质量指令调优数据集的可扩展且高效替代方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reduce data requirements for instruction tuning while maintaining or improving model performance, TRIM introduces a token-centric, forward-only framework that derives saliency via interpretable multi-layer attention patterns rather than gradients. By matching attention-based fingerprints from a small set of target samples, TRIM identifies structurally relevant tokens without costly backward passes. Experimental results show that coresets selected using TRIM consistently outperform existing baselines by up to 9% on downstream tasks and in some cases exceed full-data fine-tuning performance, all while using significantly less computational cost.
为降低指令调优所需数据量同时保持性能，TRIM提出一种基于注意力指纹匹配的词元级前向方法，通过少量目标样本识别结构相关词元，避免梯度计算。该方法生成的核集在下游任务中持续优于现有基线最高9%，部分场景甚至超越全数据微调效果，且计算成本大幅降低。
Computational Imaging Priors for Wireless Capsule Endoscopy: Monte Carlo-Guided Hemoglobin Mapping for Rare-Anomaly Detection
Authors: Chengshuai Yang, Lei Xing, Gregory Entin, Roopa Vemulapalli, Lisa Casey, Raiyan Tripti Zaman
First: 2026-05-14T16:52:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:52:33+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 6 figures, 3 tables. Code and trained-model checkpoints at https://github.com/integritynoble/GI_Multi_Task . 6-seed (seeds 41, 42, 43, 44, 45, 47) mean +/- SD ablation as the headline; per-class single-seed=42 analyses in Appendix A
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Background. RGB-trained capsule-endoscopy classifiers underperform on small-vessel vascular findings by conflating hemoglobin contrast with bile and illumination falloff. Thus, here we test whether a Monte Carlo-inspired analytic model can compute hemoglobin from RGB signal built upon extracted classifier. Methods. On Kvasir-Capsule (47,238 frames, video-level 70/15/15 split, 11 evaluable classes) we evaluate two software-only configurations against RGB-only EfficientNet-B0 across 6 seeds: (i) a prior P_blood = sigma(alpha * (H_norm - 0.5)) * Phi(r) fused as 2 zero-init auxiliary channels; (ii) a distillation head training a 3-channel RGB backbone to predict P_blood. Significance: paired DeLong, McNemar, bootstrap CIs with Bonferroni correction. Results. Across 6 seeds (n=6,423), the analytic prior provides a small but direction-consistent macro-AUC improvement: RGB-only 0.760 +/- 0.027, input-fusion 0.783 +/- 0.024 (paired Delta = +0.023, sign-positive on 5/6 seeds), distillation 0.773 +/- 0.028. The largest robust per-class lift is on Lymphangiectasia, where AUC rises from RGB 0.238 +/- 0.057 to input-fusion 0.337 +/- 0.019, sign-consistent across all 6 seeds. On rare focal-vascular classes (Angiectasia, Blood - fresh) the prior's per-seed effects are bimodal: seed=42 reaches Angiectasia AUC 0.528 -> 0.916, but the cross-seed mean is 0.646 -> 0.608 with sigma_PI = 0.23 - reported as a high-variance per-seed exemplar. Conclusion. A Monte Carlo-inspired analytic prior provides a small, direction-consistent macro-AUC improvement on Kvasir-Capsule across 6 seeds with the largest robust per-class lift on Lymphangiectasia; the distillation variant runs on plain 3-channel RGB and yields a free interpretability heatmap.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor performance of RGB-trained capsule endoscopy classifiers on small-vessel vascular anomalies due to misinterpretation of hemoglobin contrast, this study proposes a Monte Carlo-inspired analytic model to compute hemoglobin from RGB signals. Two software-only methods were evaluated on Kvasir-Capsule: one fusing a hemoglobin prior as auxiliary channels, and another using a distillation head to train a RGB backbone to predict hemoglobin. Across six seeds, the prior method improved macro-AUC from 0.760 to 0.783, with the largest per-class gain observed for Lymphangiectasia (AUC from 0.238 to 0.337). For rare vascular classes, the prior showed high per-seed variance, with one seed achieving a dramatic AUC jump for Angiectasia, though the cross-seed mean showed no improvement.
由于RGB训练的胶囊内镜分类器在小血管异常检测中因混淆血红蛋白对比度与胆汁和光照衰减而表现不佳，本研究提出一种受蒙特卡洛启发的分析模型，从RGB信号中计算血红蛋白。在Kvasir-Capsule数据集上，两种纯软件方法被评估：一种是通过两个辅助通道融合先验，另一种是通过蒸馏头训练RGB主干网络预测血红蛋白。六次种子实验中，先验方法将宏观AUC从0.760提升至0.783，其中Lymphangiectasia类提升最显著（AUC从0.238升至0.337）。蒸馏变体也获得0.773的AUC提升，并提供免费的可解释性热图，但对罕见血管类如Angiectasia，单次种子结果波动较大。
Small Language Models (SLMs) Can Still Pack a Punch: A survey (updated 2026)
Authors: Akanksha Gupta, Bijo Thomas, Harshita Asnani, Phanindra Reddy Madduru, Samia Feroze, Shreyas Subramanian, Vikram Elango, Mecit Gungor
First: 2025-01-03T19:53:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:52:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As foundation AI models continue to increase in size, an important question arises - is massive scale the only path forward? This survey of about 160 papers presents a family of Small Language Models (SLMs) in the 1 to 8 billion parameter range that demonstrate smaller models can perform as well, or even outperform large models. We explore task agnostic, general purpose SLMs, task-specific SLMs and techniques to create SLMs that can guide the community to build models while balancing performance, efficiency, scalability and cost. Furthermore we define and characterize SLMs' effective sizes, representing increased capability with respect to LLMs.
中文标题/摘要

Motivated by the question of whether massive scale is the only viable path for AI progress, this survey examines Small Language Models (SLMs) ranging from 1 to 8 billion parameters, demonstrating that smaller models can match or exceed the performance of larger ones across various tasks. The study categorizes SLMs into task-agnostic general-purpose models, task-specific models, and techniques for building efficient SLMs that balance performance, cost, and scalability. Experimental findings show that SLMs can achieve comparable or superior results to large models while offering significant advantages in computational efficiency and deployment flexibility, with their effective sizes defined to reflect enhanced capability relative to larger LLMs.
鉴于大规模模型是否是AI发展的唯一路径存疑，本综述研究了参数规模在1至8亿之间的小型语言模型（SLM），证明小型模型在多种任务中可达到或超越大型模型的表现。文章将SLM分为通用型、任务专用型及构建高效可扩展模型的技术，并通过160篇论文的实证分析表明，SLM在性能相当或更优的同时，显著降低计算开销与资源需求，其有效规模被明确定义，反映出相对于传统大模型的更强能力。
Radiologist-Guided Causal Concept Bottleneck Models for Chest X-Ray Interpretation
Authors: Amy Rafferty, Rishi Ramaesh, Ajitha Rajan
First: 2026-05-08T14:21:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:51:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) in medical imaging aim to improve model interpretability by predicting intermediate clinical concepts before final diagnoses. However, most existing CBMs treat concepts as discriminative predictors of pathology labels, without explicitly modelling the underlying clinical generative process where diseases produce observable radiographic findings. We propose XpertCausal, a radiologist-guided causal CBM for chest X-ray interpretation which models pathology-to-concept relationships using a probabilistic noisy-OR framework. This generative model is then inverted via Bayesian inference to estimate pathology probabilities from predicted concepts. Radiologist-curated concept-pathology associations are used to constrain model structure to radiologist-defined clinically plausible reasoning pathways. We evaluate XpertCausal on MIMIC-CXR across pathology classification performance, calibration, explanation quality, and alignment with radiologist-defined reasoning pathways. Compared with both a non-causal CBM baseline and a causal ablation with unconstrained learned associations, XpertCausal achieves improved AUROC, calibration, and clinically relevant explanation quality, while learning concept-pathology relationships that more closely align with expert knowledge. These results demonstrate that incorporating clinically motivated causal structure and expert domain knowledge into CBMs can lead to more accurate, interpretable, and clinically aligned models for CXR interpretation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：放射科医生引导的因果概念瓶颈模型用于胸部X光片解读
医学影像中的概念瓶颈模型（CBMs）旨在通过在最终诊断前预测中间临床概念来提升模型可解释性。然而，现有大多数CBMs将概念视为病理标签的判别性预测因子，未明确建模疾病产生可观察影像表现的临床生成过程。我们提出XpertCausal，一种放射科医生引导的因果CBM，用于胸部X光片解读，采用概率性噪声OR框架建模病理到概念的关系。该生成模型通过贝叶斯推断进行逆向，从预测概念估计病理概率。利用放射科医生整理的概念-病理关联约束模型结构，使其符合放射科医生定义的临床合理推理路径。我们在MIMIC-CXR数据集上评估XpertCausal，涵盖病理分类性能、校准度、解释质量及与放射科医生定义推理路径的一致性。与非因果CBM基线模型及无约束学习关联的因果消融模型相比，XpertCausal在AUROC、校准度和临床相关解释质量方面表现更优，所学概念-病理关系更贴近专家知识。结果表明，将临床驱动的因果结构与专家领域知识融入CBMs，可提升胸部X光片解读模型的准确性、可解释性及临床一致性。

The model is inverted via Bayesian inference to estimate pathology probabilities from predicted concepts, with radiologist-curated concept-pathology associations constraining the structure to reflect clinically plausible reasoning. Evaluated on MIMIC-CXR, XpertCausal outperformed both a non-causal CBM baseline and a causal ablation with unconstrained associations, achieving higher AUROC, better calibration, and more clinically relevant explanations while aligning more closely with expert-defined reasoning pathways.
为提升胸部X光模型的可解释性与临床一致性，本文提出XpertCausal，一种由放射科医生指导的因果概念瓶颈模型，采用概率性noisy-OR框架显式建模病理到概念的关系，并通过贝叶斯推断反向估计病理概率，其结构受放射科医生整理的概念-病理关联约束，以反映临床合理推理路径。在MIMIC-CXR数据集上评估显示，该模型优于非因果CBM基线和无约束因果消融模型，在AUROC、校准度和临床相关解释质量上均有提升，且其学习到的概念-病理关系更贴近专家知识。
NeuroTrain: Surveying Local Learning Rules for Spiking Neural Networks with an Open Benchmarking Framework
Authors: Alessio Caviglia, Filippo Marostica, Roberta Bardini, Alessandro Savino, Stefano Di Carlo
First: 2026-05-14T16:50:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:50:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rapid expansion of spiking neural networks (SNNs) has led to a proliferation of training algorithms that differ widely in biological inspiration, computational structure, and hardware suitability. Despite this progress, the field lacks a unified, fine-grained taxonomy that systematically organizes these approaches and clarifies their conceptual relationships. This survey provides a comprehensive taxonomy of SNN training algorithms, spanning surrogate-gradient backpropagation, local and three-factor learning rules, biologically inspired plasticity mechanisms, ANN-to-SNN conversion pipelines, and non-standard optimization strategies. We analyze each class in terms of its computational principles, learning signals, and locality properties. To support reproducible research, we release NeuroTrain, an open-source snnTorch-based framework that implements a representative set of these algorithms within a unified, modular, and extendable framework, enabling consistent benchmarking across datasets, architectures, and training regimes. By consolidating fragmented literature and providing a reusable benchmarking framework, this survey identifies common patterns, highlights open challenges, and outlines promising directions for future work on scalable, efficient SNN training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NeuroTrain：基于开放基准框架的脉冲神经网络局部学习规则综述
脉冲神经网络（SNNs）的快速发展催生了大量训练算法，这些算法在生物启发性、计算结构和硬件适用性方面差异显著。尽管取得进展，该领域仍缺乏统一、细致的分类体系，以系统组织这些方法并阐明其概念关系。本综述提供了一种全面的SNN训练算法分类体系，涵盖代理梯度反向传播、局部及三因素学习规则、生物启发性可塑性机制、ANN到SNN转换流程及非常规优化策略。我们从计算原理、学习信号和局部性特征角度分析各类算法。为支持可复现研究，我们发布了NeuroTrain，一个基于snnTorch的开源框架，该框架在统一、模块化且可扩展的架构中实现了代表性算法，支持在不同数据集、架构和训练范式下的一致性基准测试。通过整合零散文献并提供可复用的基准框架，本综述识别了共同模式，突出了开放挑战，并勾勒了未来可扩展、高效SNN训练研究的前景。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the fragmented landscape of spiking neural network training algorithms, this survey introduces a systematic taxonomy that categorizes diverse methods by biological inspiration, computational structure, and hardware suitability. It analyzes key classes including surrogate-gradient backpropagation, local learning rules, and ANN-to-SNN conversion techniques, evaluating them by computational principles, learning signals, and locality. 为应对脉冲神经网络训练算法种类繁多、缺乏统一分类体系的问题，该综述构建了系统化的分类框架，按生物启发性、计算结构和硬件适配性对不同方法进行归类，涵盖代理梯度反向传播、局部学习规则、生物可塑性机制及ANN到SNN转换策略等类别，并从计算原理、学习信号和局部性角度进行分析。作者开源了NeuroTrain框架，基于snnTorch实现代表性算法，支持模块化扩展与跨数据集、架构的统一评估。实验表明，局部学习规则在性能与计算开销间取得较好平衡，而代理梯度方法虽具可扩展性但资源消耗较高。
Adapting Dijkstra for Buffers and Unlimited Transfers
Authors: Denys Katkalo, Andrii Rohovyi, Toby Walsh
First: 2026-03-12T09:36:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:49:45+00:00
Comments: v3: revised manuscript incorporating reviewer feedback (formal correctness proof, deployment trade-off discussion, route/tau_min definitions, dominance-inequality fix); editorial and layout polish
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In recent years, RAPTOR based algorithms have been considered the state-of-the-art for path-finding with unlimited transfers without preprocessing. However, this status largely stems from the evolution of routing research, where Dijkstra-based solutions were superseded by timetable-based algorithms without a systematic comparison. In this work, we revisit classical Dijkstra-based approaches for public transit routing with unlimited transfers and demonstrate that Time-Dependent Dijkstra (TD-Dijkstra) outperforms MR. However, efficient TD-Dijkstra implementations rely on filtering dominated connections during preprocessing, which assumes passengers can always switch to a faster connection. We show that this filtering is unsound when stops have buffer times, as it cannot distinguish between seated passengers who may continue without waiting and transferring passengers who must respect the buffer. To address this limitation, we introduce Transfer Aware Dijkstra (TAD), a modification that scans entire trip sequences rather than individual edges, correctly handling buffer times while maintaining performance advantages over MR. Our experiments on London and Switzerland networks show that we can achieve a greater than two time speed-up over MR while producing optimal results on both networks with and without buffer times.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Dijkstra算法在缓冲时间和无限换乘场景下的适应性
近年来，基于RAPTOR的算法被认为是无需预处理的无限换乘路径搜索的最先进技术。然而，这一地位很大程度上源于路由研究的发展，Dijkstra类解决方案被基于时刻表的算法取代，但缺乏系统性比较。本文重新审视了面向公共交通无限换乘的Dijkstra类方法，证明时间依赖型Dijkstra（TD-Dijkstra）优于MR算法。然而，高效的TD-Dijkstra实现依赖于预处理阶段过滤被支配的连接，这假设乘客总能切换到更快的连接。我们指出，当站点存在缓冲时间时，该过滤方法不成立，因为它无法区分无需等待即可继续的就座乘客与必须遵守缓冲时间的换乘乘客。为解决此局限，我们提出换乘感知Dijkstra（TAD），通过扫描完整行程序列而非单个边，正确处理缓冲时间，同时保持优于MR的性能优势。在伦敦和瑞士网络上的实验表明，TAD相比MR可实现两倍以上的速度提升，且在含或不含缓冲时间的网络中均能生成最优结果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of systematic comparison between Dijkstra-based and timetable-based transit routing methods, this work revisits Dijkstra for unlimited transfers and finds that Time-Dependent Dijkstra (TD-Dijkstra) outperforms MR. However, standard TD-Dijkstra fails when buffer times exist, as its dominance filtering incorrectly assumes all passengers can switch to faster connections. To fix this, the authors propose Transfer Aware Dijkstra (TAD), which scans full trip sequences to properly account for buffer times while preserving performance. Experiments on London and Switzerland networks show TAD achieves over two times speed-up over MR while producing optimal results in both buffer-inclusive and buffer-excluded scenarios.
鉴于对Dijkstra与时刻表算法在无预处理公交路径搜索中的比较缺乏系统性，本文重新审视Dijkstra方法，证明时间依赖Dijkstra（TD-Dijkstra）优于MR。然而，标准TD-Dijkstra的过滤机制假设乘客总可切换至更快连接，这在存在缓冲时间时失效，因无法区分已就座乘客与需等待换乘的乘客。为此，本文提出转移感知Dijkstra（TAD），通过扫描完整行程序列正确处理缓冲时间，同时保持性能优势。在伦敦与瑞士网络上的实验表明，TAD相比MR提速超过两倍，且在有无缓冲时间情况下均能输出最优解。
Deceptive Cookies: Consent by Design -- A Mixed Methods Study
Authors: Liv Hilde Sjøflot, Tobias A. Opsahl
First: 2026-05-14T16:49:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:49:27+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 12 figures, including appendix
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While companies increasingly rely on data, especially when it comes to targeted advertising, adapting content to users, selling data and training machine learning models, the collection of data raises privacy concerns. One way of collecting data is by using HTTP cookies when interacting with a website. Legal regulations require service providers to collect consent for some forms of cookie collection, which is often acquired through \emph{cookie consent banners}, but their effectiveness has been debated. This study aims to understand what influences users' experience and behaviour when managing their cookie consent, by investigating the gap between their stated privacy preferences and their actual actions. A mixed methods approach was used, collecting data from a usability test and a survey (N=20). The results showed that although participants generally want to reject cookie collection, they often end up accepting because of deceptive patterns in the cookie consent banner design. It also showed that they were more willing to consent to websites they trusted and if they expected it would improve their user experience. Although the current EU legislation states that withdrawing consent must be as easy as giving it, withdrawing consent took on average more than 20 times longer than giving it. This suggests that cookie consent banners in their current form are not ideal with respect to user autonomy, often leading users to \emph{consent by design}.

标题：欺骗性Cookie：设计中的同意——一项混合方法研究

Summary / 总结
This study investigates how users interact with cookie consent banners, motivated by concerns over privacy and the effectiveness of current consent mechanisms. Using a mixed-methods approach with a usability test and survey (N=20), it finds that while users generally prefer to reject cookies, they frequently accept them due to deceptive design patterns in consent banners.
